### [1] [Memoir: Learning, Verifying, and Evolving False-Positive Memories for Static Application Security Testing Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09181)
*Shenyuan Guan,Qiaodan Hou,Yanjun Chen,Xincheng Wen,Jia Feng,Keke Lian,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Memoir：一种面向静态应用安全测试告警的记忆驱动误报识别框架。该方法将历史误报告警提炼为可复用、可持续演化的语义记忆，并在新告警上检索、验证和更新这些记忆，在CWE-Bench-Java上取得99.43%的F1值、98.88%的召回率和100%的精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态应用安全测试工具会产生大量误报告警，导致人工审查成本高、开发者信任下降。现有方法难以跨工具和跨漏洞类别学习稳定的误报模式，且通常依赖静态知识，无法利用后续人工验证或预测确认后不断积累的新案例更新模型知识。

Method: Memoir包含两个核心阶段：其一，历史语义记忆构建，利用大语言模型引导标注、模式聚类和记忆综合，将历史误报告警转化为结构化语义记忆，以抽取可迁移的行为模式；其二，记忆驱动识别与演化，对新告警检索相关记忆，并结合分类体系一致性与安全不变量进行语义验证后输出误报预测。经验证的预测案例会被回写至记忆库，使知识库随案例积累持续演化。

Result: 在CWE-Bench-Java评测中，Memoir获得99.43%的F1值、98.88%的召回率和100%的精确率，并稳定优于所比较的基线方法。来自一家大型IT企业生产软件系统的工业案例研究表明，其学习得到的记忆库能够跨不同静态分析工具泛化，且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: Memoir通过将历史误报转化为可检索、可验证、可增量更新的语义记忆，有效缓解了传统误报消减方案对固定规则或静态训练数据的依赖。其主要贡献在于将大语言模型辅助的知识抽取、语义验证与持续记忆演化结合起来，并展示了较强的跨工具泛化能力；但摘要未说明数据集规模、基线构成、误报标签来源及记忆更新错误可能带来的累积偏差，仍需结合全文进一步评估其稳健性与可复现性。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools have become indispensable in modern secure software devel- opment. However, these tools often generate false-positive (FP) alerts, imposing substantial manual inspection costs and reducing the trust from developers. Existing FP reduction methods still face two primary challenges. First, the large differences among SAST tools and vulnerability categories make it difficult for these methods to learn recurring patterns in historical false positives. Moreover, the knowledge used by these methods are largely static and cannot be updated as newly validated cases accumulate. To address these challenges, we propose Memoir, a memory- driven framework for identifying false positives by transform- ing historical FP alerts into reusable semantic memories. It consists of two key modules. First, historical semantic memory construction converts historical FP alerts into structured semantic memories through LLM-guided annotation, pattern clustering, and memory synthesis to capture reusable behavioral patterns. Moreover, memory-driven identification and evolution retrieves relevant memories and performs semantic verification against taxonomy consistency and security invariants before making the final prediction. It then incorporates verified predictions back into the memory repository, allowing the knowledge base to evolve as new cases accumulate. We evaluate Memoir on CWE- Bench-Java to demonstrate its effectiveness in real-world security analysis. Specifically, Memoir achieves an F1-score of 99.43% with a Recall of 98.88% and perfect Precision, consistently outperforming other baselines. Furthermore, an industrial case study on production software systems from a top IT company shows that the learned memory base generalizes effectively across different SAST tools without retraining.

</details>

### [2] [Zero-Trust Federated Learning for Connected Aftermarket Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07591)
*Shunmukha Sagar Puppala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ZT FL CADE，将零信任访问控制、隐私保护联邦学习与对抗性验证结合，用于联网汽车后市场设备的预测性维护和OTA更新安全。基于单一合成数据集的实验显示，其维护风险F1、入侵检测F1和对抗客户端下的召回率均优于基线，同时满足低时延要求，但尚无真实场景验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网后市场设备来源多样、所有权分散且服役周期长，难以依赖传统网络边界进行统一防护。现有研究通常将零信任策略与联邦学习分开处理，缺少面向OTA更新、设备访问和预测维护的联合安全框架。

Method: 构建ZT FL CADE边缘学习架构：设备侧训练时序模型，通过隐私约束的联邦聚合共享模型更新；结合设备行为、更新完整性和信任证据进行风险评分；针对更新请求执行允许、质询或隔离决策。实验使用来自240台设备、12个供应商域、180天运行周期的144000个合成遥测窗口，预测维护风险、更新入侵和访问动作，并设置部分恶意客户端测试鲁棒性。

Result: 相较FedAvg，维护风险F1由0.837提升至0.883，入侵检测F1由0.856提升至0.897；当被选客户端中20%具有对抗性时，入侵检测召回率仍为0.842。平均访问决策延迟为44毫秒，低于模拟设定的100毫秒运行预算。主要局限是数据集为单一合成数据集，未报告真实车辆、真实设备异构性或跨环境泛化结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，零信任策略执行与联邦学习可以在联网后市场设备场景中进行联合建模和评估，并可能同时改善安全检测性能与访问控制效率。但当前证据仅支持仿真层面的可行性，仍需通过多来源真实数据、实际OTA流程、不同攻击策略及现场部署测试验证其实用性。

Abstract: Connected aftermarket devices extend vehicle diagnostics, repair workflows, and over-the-air software maintenance beyond original equipment manufacturer boundaries, yet their heterogeneous ownership and long service life complicate conventional perimeter security. This paper develops Zero Trust Federated Learning for Connected Aftermarket Devices (ZT FL CADE), an edge-learning architecture that combines device-level access control, privacy-preserving federated learning, and adversarial validation for over-the-air update and predictive maintenance decisions. The evaluation uses a single synthetic dataset of 144,000 telemetry windows from 240 devices, 12 vendor domains, and 180 days of operation. Features include bus entropy, update latency, attestation age, signature retries, environmental signals, fault-code rates, packet loss, drift, mileage, and trust score, with targets for maintenance risk, update intrusion, and access action. ZT FL CADE trains local temporal models, aggregates privacy-bounded updates, scores each device against behavioral and update integrity evidence, and routes update requests to allow, challenge, or quarantine actions. Synthetic experiments improve maintenance risk F1 from 0.837 for Fed Avg to 0.883, improve intrusion F1 from 0.856 to 0.897, and preserve 0.842 intrusion recall when 20 percent of selected clients are adversarial. Mean access-decision latency remains 44 MS, below the 100 MS operational budget used in the simulation. The results do not establish field validation, but they indicate that zero-trust policy enforcement and federated learning can be evaluated jointly rather than as separate aftermarket security controls. Index Terms Zero trust architecture, federated learning, connected aftermarket devices, over-the-air updates, adversarial machine learning, edge artificial intelligence, predictive maintenance, automotive cybersecurity

</details>


### [3] [Adversarial Attacks on Deep OCR Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07636)
*Wenbo Sun,Hongzong LI,Yanyun Wang,Jiahao MA,Shuxin Zhuang,Rong Feng,Shiqin Tang,Zi Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Deep-OCR (DeepSeek-OCR) advances document recognition by treating the visual modality as an optical compression medium, enabling long-context OCR at low token cost. However, its increased complexity may introduce new security vulnerabilities. In this paper, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first pure black-box adversarial attack against a generative OCR vision-language model, where only the decoded string can be queried and no gradients, logits, or model internals are available. We recast the attack as a zeroth-order optimization problem driven by a bounded scalar loss defined directly on the string output via sequence similarity, and estimate the gradient with a random-direction finite-difference scheme whose query cost is independent of the image dimension. An Adam update with ell_infinity projection yields imperceptible perturbations for both untargeted and targeted objectives. Pilot experiments on Deep-OCR validate the string-only attack and evaluation pipeline and expose severe qualitative decoder failures, including repetition, truncation, and prompt leakage. They also show that controlled targeted rewriting remains substantially harder than untargeted degradation; we avoid claiming targeted success until the pre-registered evaluation is complete.

</details>


### [4] [Can AI Write Compliant Code, and to What Extent? Evaluating SOC 2 Compliance of Claude Fable 5, Claude Opus 4.8, and Claude Opus 5 Across Four Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07776)
*Iccha Sethi,Herman Errico*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究评估语言模型在未提示和提示SOC 2标准两种条件下生成代码时的安全控制符合度。结果显示，未提示时符合度为47%—88%，加入一句SOC 2提示后提升至86%—100%，但仍无法覆盖模型未纳入任务理解的控制；同时，基于模式匹配的自动评分器存在明显可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产团队 increasingly 使用语言模型生成涉及存储、身份认证、凭据和受监管数据的代码，但模型是否会主动实施加密、访问限制、日志记录、数据保留和多因素认证等SOC 2相关控制尚不明确。研究还希望判断，仅在提示中加入标准名称能否有效改善安全性。

Method: 选取3个前沿模型、4类应用场景（S3命令行工具、认证服务、RDS Terraform模块和个人数据文件上传处理器），分别使用中性任务描述和附加一句SOC 2要求的提示各生成一次，共24个输出。研究者依据映射到具体Trust Services Criteria的二元评分量表进行评估，并人工复核所有失败项和被标记的行为；同时比较模式匹配评分与语义评分的一致性。

Result: 中性提示下的控制符合度为47%—88%，通常写法中固有的控制（如密码哈希和storage_encrypted）较常出现，而S3强化配置、数据保留和MFA接口等控制常被遗漏。中性提示还产生了可利用的安全缺陷，包括可访问的调试器、未认证下载接口以及返回全部姓名和邮箱的端点，且初始检查表未能识别部分缺陷。加入一句SOC 2要求后，各场景得分提升至86%—100%，提高23—50个百分点，并消除了所观察到的不安全构造，但MFA接口、Cookie安全属性和账户生命周期管理等超出模型任务理解范围的控制仍可能缺失。模型选择影响较小；模式匹配评分器在216次判断中有27次与语义评分不一致，并放过了一个真实缺陷。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够响应高层安全标准提示，但默认不会系统性实施完整的合规控制。SOC 2提示可显著减少常见安全缺陷，却不能替代明确的控制需求、人工审查和面向语义的自动验证。未来评估应扩展控制覆盖范围，并采用语义检查而非单纯模式匹配，以识别条件性实现、可达性和实际安全影响。

Abstract: Software teams now delegate production code to language models, including code that provisions storage, handles credentials, and stores regulated data, so we asked whether a model applies the controls a SOC~2 program expects (encryption, restricted access, logging, retention) when nobody mentions security, and how much one sentence naming the standard changes the answer. We tested three frontier models (Claude Fable~5, Opus~4.8, and Opus~5) across four use cases (an S3 CLI, an authentication service, an RDS Terraform module, and a file-upload handler holding personal data), each generated once from a neutral task statement and once with a single SOC~2 sentence added, scoring all 24 outputs against binary rubrics mapped to specific Trust Services Criteria and hand-verifying every failure and flagged act. Unprompted conformance ran from 47\% to 88\% and tracked whether a control is part of how the code is normally written, so password hashing and \texttt{storage\_encrypted} appear unasked while S3 hardening calls, retention, and MFA hooks do not. The neutral prompt also shipped real vulnerabilities, including a reachable Werkzeug debugger allowing remote code execution, an unauthenticated download, and an endpoint returning every stored name and email, all scored clean by our first checklist, with a fourth defect passing because its value was computed by a conditional. One SOC~2 sentence moved every case to 86--100\%, worth 23 to 50 points, and removed every insecure construction, though controls outside the model's conception of the task survived it, including MFA hooks, cookie flags, and account lifecycle. Model choice mattered least, with same-generation models within one rubric item across all eight cells, and the pattern-matching scorer proved unreliable, disagreeing with semantic grading on 27 of 216 judgments and passing a real defect, so it needs replacing with semantic checks.

</details>


### [5] [The Anatomy of a Prompt Injection: A Component Model for Structured Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07808)
*Jeremy McHugh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于结构化描述提示注入攻击的七组件模型，将攻击从表面字符串匹配提升为基于攻击意图、工具目标、作用汇和执行效果的系统化标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入研究主要记录具体文本，难以应对自然语言变体、代理能力增强及嵌入式攻击。作者旨在帮助防御者、红队和网络威胁情报团队统一标注、比较和变异攻击样本，并提高与现有CTI体系的兼容性。

Method: 提出由载体、投递向量、隐蔽机制、上下文突破、权限提升、有效载荷和返回通道组成的七组件模型，其中前五项被归为攻击工件字段，后两项属于环境字段。论文制定标注规则，构建可映射至行业CTI模式的逻辑分析记录，并通过EchoLeak（CVE-2025-32711）、真实环境中的恶意软件AI规避样本及代理流程图进行说明，同时对比HOUYI、Promptware Kill Chain、活动分类法和ReNeLLM等相关框架。

Result: 论文形成了一个能够抽象不同自然语言实现、追踪攻击者意图及其可执行效果的统一描述框架。该框架覆盖提示注入的关键结构要素，可将既有方法视为部分覆盖或受限投影，并提供了可操作的实例化标注方案。摘要未报告定量实验、检测准确率或与基线方法的系统性能比较。

Conclusion: 该工作将提示注入从脆弱的字符串级分析推进到结构化、意图导向的攻击建模，为跨样本比较、威胁情报共享、红队测试和防御规则设计提供了统一基础。其实际价值取决于标注一致性、复杂代理环境中的可扩展性，以及后续对检测效果和跨场景泛化能力的实证验证。

Abstract: Four years after prompt injection was first identified in 2022, attacks are still predominantly documented as verbatim strings rather than structured exploits, despite advancing agent capabilities and threat actors embedding injections to subvert AI-assisted security analysis. This paper formalizes the structure of prompt-injection artifacts, enabling defenders, red teamers, and cyber threat intelligence (CTI) teams to label, compare, and mutate attacks without relying on fragile string matching. Because large language models compile varied natural-language realizations into identical executable actions, labeling must track attacker intent (tool targets, sinks, and effects) rather than surface wording. We propose a seven-component model (carrier, delivery vector, concealment, context-break, privilege escalation, payload, and return channel) consisting of five artifact fields and two environment fields. This framework unifies roles partially addressed by HOUYI's payload decomposition, the Promptware Kill Chain, and campaign taxonomies, while framing minimal jailbreak frameworks like ReNeLLM as projections onto a restricted subspace. We provide clear labeling rules, a logical analysis record mapping directly to industry CTI schemas, worked examples including EchoLeak (CVE-2025-32711) and an in-the-wild malware AI-evasion sample, and an illustrative agentic flowchart.

</details>


### [6] [China RealDID: Verifiable Credentials Anchored in Legal Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07846)
*Yifan He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Verifiable credentials (VCs) and decentralized identifiers (DIDs) enable selective disclosure but lack legal anchoring: without a trusted identity root, verifiers cannot distinguish a genuine holder from a fabricated identity. State identity systems provide biometric-grounded verification but impose three costs: verifiers must collect subjects' full personally identifiable information, infrastructure concentrates on a single API, and the state observes every transaction. We present China RealDID, a three-layer architecture -- CTID (centralized legal identity), RealDID (decentralized anchor on an open permissioned blockchain), and VCs with SD-JWT-based selective disclosure -- evaluated against five adversary classes and six security goals. The central mechanism is a content-blind government relay: the state authenticates participants and counter-signs every credential but cannot read the payload, encrypted by the issuer to the holder's public key. We describe the VC lifecycle, triple-signature chain, open template registry, and the architecture's metadata-level privacy limits, including the credential graph at the relay and presentation linkability from single-DID reuse. The design yields an asymmetric, state-bounded trust model: the state cannot impersonate or read contents; the user cannot fabricate identity or evade metadata observation. We analyze alignment with China's Personal Information Protection Law and the EU's GDPR, including the tension between immutable registries and erasure rights, and discuss generalizability through cross-border deployments with Singapore and Hong Kong.

</details>


### [7] [Capability-Routed Guard: Defending Large Reasoning Models Against Reasoning-Centric Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07892)
*Yiyong Liu,Yixin Wu,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Capability-Routed Guard（CRG），一种面向闭源大型推理模型的推理时防护框架，通过将提示防御建模为“能力路由”问题，以降低针对推理上下文、任务分解和能力解释的对抗性攻击风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型会暴露推理过程相关的新型安全失效模式：攻击者可将不当目标伪装成正常推理步骤。现有安全提醒、外部分类器和自检机制通常直接处理对抗提示，或要求目标模型在同一易受攻击表面上继续进行安全推理，因此容易失效。对于闭源模型，防御方又无法查看隐藏推理轨迹或修改参数，因而需要模型无关的外部防护机制。

Method: CRG在侧信道控制器中构建可信任务表示，显式提取用户被授权的任务、当前有效上下文、安全证据及能力迁移风险，并将可执行意图与不可信推理上下文分离。系统随后进行分路径处置：对高风险请求予以拦截，对模糊请求施加约束，并将低风险请求结合可信上下文转发。最后，CRG通过TraceCheck验证输出或执行过程与授权任务的一致性；若验证或路由不满足条件，则调用受限回退机制，以尽量维持低风险正常请求的可用性。

Result: 实验表明，CRG能够有效缓解多种以推理机制为目标的越狱攻击，同时保持正常任务效用，并减少常见的过度拒答现象。消融与进一步分析显示，可信表示、能力路由、TraceCheck和受限回退等组件具有互补作用，联合使用优于单一防护措施。

Conclusion: 该工作将闭源大型推理模型的提示防御从单纯内容识别转向任务授权与能力路由控制。其核心贡献在于：不依赖模型权重修改或隐藏推理链访问，通过可信上下文隔离、风险分流和一致性验证实现更稳健的推理时安全防护，并在安全性与正常可用性之间取得较好的平衡。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) expose a new safety failure mode: adversarial prompts can manipulate reasoning context, task decomposition, or capability interpretation so that harmful objectives are processed as legitimate reasoning steps. Existing safeguards, including safety reminders, external classifiers, and self-checking wrappers, are often brittle because they either inspect the adversarial prompt directly or ask the target model to perform additional safety reasoning on the same surface that attacks exploit. We introduce Capability-Routed Guard (CRG), a model-agnostic inference-time guardrail for closed-source LRMs, where defenders cannot inspect hidden reasoning traces or modify model weights. CRG reframes prompt defense as a capability-routing problem: a side-channel controller first constructs a trusted representation of the user's authorized task, active context, safety evidence, and capability-transfer risk, separating executable intent from untrusted reasoning context. This representation supports route-specific execution, allowing CRG to block high-risk requests, constrain ambiguous ones, and forward low-risk requests through trusted active context. Finally, CRG applies TraceCheck to verify consistency with the authorized task and invokes a restricted fallback to preserve utility for low-risk benign prompts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CRG effectively mitigates diverse reasoning-centric jailbreaks while preserving benign utility and avoiding common over-refusal issues. Further analysis shows that its components contribute complementary benefits, highlighting the importance of coordinated defense mechanisms for securing large reasoning models.

</details>


### [8] [Private Anytime Selective-Risk Certification for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Guarantees and Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07913)
*Sanjeda Akter,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fed-SRC，一种面向联邦学习、差分隐私、自适应监测和检索增强生成的分数无关选择性风险证书。方法可在多轮、多个阈值及可选停止条件下控制风险与接受量的同时置信界，但实际认证能力高度依赖任务、评分函数和目标风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性风险证书通常难以同时处理联邦数据、差分隐私噪声、自适应阈值选择、动态招募与退出以及随时停止。直接对非隐私证书进行加噪会破坏其统计保证，因此需要一种兼具隐私性、联邦性和任意时刻有效性的认证框架。

Method: 客户端仅发布加入高斯噪声的分数直方图和损失直方图。Fed-SRC结合按记录索引和按噪声方差索引的鞅，对所有已登记阈值与轮次上的目标风险对比量和接受样本量进行联合控制，从而支持可预测招募、掉线、阈值选择和可选停止。方法还使用范围为1的总变差项，将校准混合分布转移到预先声明的部署混合分布。作者另外评估了一个未证明e-process有效性的私有投注资本启发式方法。

Result: 在所有评估任务、隐私水平和策略单元中均未观察到同时置信界被违反。主要目标风险r*=0.10从未获得认证；在RAGTruth上，次级目标r*=0.20也从未获得认证；在HaluEval问答任务中，200次非私有试验全部获得认证，且留出风险低于目标。直接对非私有证书进行朴素隐私化后，200次试验中有146至198次违反界限。启发式方法在epsilon≤4时停止认证，而Fed-SRC仍可认证；不过认证约需30倍于独立校准样本数的流事件。

Conclusion: Fed-SRC的主要贡献是将隐私、联邦、任意时刻和自适应选择性风险认证统一起来，而不是提出新的风险对比统计量或接受量下限。实验支持其统计有效性，但也表明认证能力并非普遍存在，显著受评分函数、数据分布、任务难度和隐私预算影响；同时其较高的事件开销限制了实际部署效率。

Abstract: Selective-risk certificates promise that accepted outputs meet a declared error target. We develop Fed-SRC, a score-agnostic certificate for federated, differentially private, adaptively monitored retrieval-augmented generation. Clients release only Gaussian-perturbed score and loss histograms. Record-indexed and noise-variance-indexed martingales jointly bound target-risk contrast and accepted mass over all registered thresholds and rounds, permitting predictable recruitment, dropout, threshold selection, and optional stopping. A range-one total-variation term transfers the calibration mixture to a declared deployment mixture. The contribution is this private, federated, anytime combination, rather than the contrast statistic or acceptance floor individually. Empirically, no simultaneous-bound violation occurs in any evaluated cell, privacy level, or policy. Operational power depends on the score and population: the primary target r*=0.10 never certifies, and on RAGTruth the secondary target r*=0.20 never certifies either, whereas on HaluEval question answering it certifies in all 200 non-private trials, with held-out risk below the target. Naively privatized non-private certificates violate their bounds in 146 to 198 of 200 trials. As an exploratory comparison, we also evaluate a private betting-capital heuristic for which we do not establish e-process validity. This heuristic stops certifying at epsilon <= 4, where Fed-SRC still certifies. Certification nevertheless consumes roughly 30 times more stream events than unique calibration items.

</details>


### [9] [EvoTrustRAG: Evolution-Aware Conflict Attribution and Evidence Handling for Reliable Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07933)
*Xi Nie,Hongwei Li,Shenghao Wu,Wenshu Fan,Qiyang Song,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出EvoTrustRAG，用于在生成答案前识别检索证据冲突的来源，而非简单选择“更可信”的事实。该方法可区分知识演化、干预性信息与无法确定的冲突，并在多个指标上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG冲突处理通常将矛盾视为静态不一致，倾向于直接筛选可信证据，因而难以区分合法的知识更新、潜在的信息干预和真实的不确定性。论文将冲突来源归因定义为独立问题，以提升动态环境中的证据处理与答案可靠性。

Method: EvoTrustRAG是一种无需训练的推理框架。它首先将带有文本跨度定位的检索事实构建为冲突证据图，然后结合时间关系、支持结构和辅助一致性，评估知识演化与方向性干预两类假设；随后将局部判断投射为每个冲突组的全局一致解释。根据归因结果，系统保留具有时间顺序的早期与后期状态，将疑似干预证据与主上下文分离，或将无法解决的冲突显式保留给生成器。

Result: 在基准原生冲突设置上，EvoTrustRAG取得81.4%的平均准确率；相较最强基线，冲突归因宏平均F1由72.2%提升至79.1%。在最强协同干预场景下，错误率由31.2%降至16.0%，表明其在冲突识别和风险控制方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该工作将RAG冲突处理从静态事实选择推进到冲突来源归因，能够更细致地处理知识演化、疑似干预和未决不确定性。实验结果支持其有效性，但摘要未说明数据规模、冲突类型分布及消融实验，因此其跨领域泛化能力和各组件的独立贡献仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models with external knowledge, yet conflicting evidence remains a fundamental challenge in dynamic and adversarial environments. Existing approaches often treat conflicts as static inconsistencies and select more reliable knowledge, overlooking that the same conflict may arise from legitimate knowledge evolution, malicious manipulation, or unresolved uncertainty. We formulate conflict origin attribution as a new problem in RAG: identifying which explanation of conflicting evidence is supported by observable context rather than simply which fact should be trusted. We propose EvoTrustRAG, a training-free framework for evolution-aware conflict attribution and evidence handling before answer generation. EvoTrustRAG represents span-grounded retrieved facts as a conflict evidence graph, evaluates grounded evolution and directional intervention hypotheses using temporal relations, support structure, and auxiliary consistency, and projects local decisions onto a globally consistent explanation of each conflict group. The attribution determines whether earlier and later states are preserved as temporal knowledge, an intervention candidate is separated from the primary context, or an unresolved conflict remains visible to the generator. Unlike provenance-based approaches focused on post-hoc analysis, EvoTrustRAG determines during inference whether conflicting evidence follows plausible knowledge evolution, exhibits intervention-like support, or cannot be reliably attributed. Experiments show that EvoTrustRAG achieves 81.4% average accuracy on benchmark-native conflict settings, improves attribution macro-F1 from 72.2% to 79.1% over the strongest baseline, and reduces the error rate under the strongest coordinated attack from 31.2% to 16.0%.

</details>

Abstract: With digital technologies now being part of the fabric of our societies, identifying and managing cybersecurity threats becomes imperative. Within the European Union, several initiatives are underway, aiming to motivate, regulate and eventually orchestrate the establishment of capacity and enhancement of situational awareness, incident response, and preparedness capabilities, with an expected emphasis on operators of essential services and state actors entrusted with cybersecurity. In this context, the institution of cooperation and information exchange channels to allow for coordinated cross-border responses to large-scale incidents is particularly prioritised. Motivated by the above, this work presents a conceptual blueprint in support of architecting and establishing interoperable Cyber Security Operations Centres that combine capacity for situational awareness, incident response, and preparedness, also benefiting from the interplay between them, ultimately enhancing national cybersecurity capabilities, cross-border collaboration, and national supervision of their critical sectors, in line with current and upcoming regulatory requirements and the ever-increasing need for national and international cooperation.

</details>


### [11] [BASIS: Breach-Aware Selective Prompt Injection Shielding with Prefill Attention Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08027)
*Laiqiao Qin,Tianqing Zhu,Longxiang Gao,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出BASIS，一种面向鲁棒性的提示注入防御方法。它不仅检测输入是否含有注入，还预测注入是否会实际破坏模型行为，从而在保持高检测率的同时减少不必要拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只要检测到提示注入就拒答，忽略不同指令模板和模型对注入攻击的抵抗能力差异，导致大量本可安全处理的输入被过度拒绝。论文旨在实现更细粒度、风险感知的防御。

Method: BASIS以注意力竞争比ρ为特征，训练两个稀疏线性探针：存在性探针用于判断是否存在提示注入，突破探针用于预测注入是否会实际影响模型行为。系统采用级联门控决策，并依次执行注入存在性检测、样本级突破预测和指令鲁棒性评估；在线阶段无需额外的大语言模型推理，仅在预测模型可能被攻破时拒答。

Result: 在四项任务和六个开源大语言模型上的实验表明，BASIS能够维持近乎完美的注入检测性能，并显著降低安全攻击样本上的过度拒答，尤其是在采用鲁棒指令模板时效果更明显。

Conclusion: 提示注入防御不应仅判断攻击是否存在，还应评估攻击是否能够真正突破模型行为约束。BASIS通过鲁棒性感知和级联探针实现了检测准确性与可用性的平衡，为减少过度拒答提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Prompt injection is a critical security threat in large language model (LLM) applications, where attackers hijack model behavior by embedding malicious instructions in user or external data. Existing detection methods only detect the presence of injection and refuse to respond upon detection, overlooking the fact that for many modern aligned models, well-crafted instructions can resist most injection attacks. This means that the injection robustness varies significantly across instructions and models. This leads to widespread unnecessary over-refusal: inputs containing injections that the model could have handled correctly are rejected incorrectly. To deal with this over-refusal issue, we propose BASIS (Robustness-Aware Prompt Injection Defense). This defense method uses the Attention Competition Ratio ($ρ$) as features to train two sparse linear probes: an existence probe and a breach probe. Both probes make defense decisions through cascaded gating, which does not require additional LLM inference. BASIS comprises three stages: injection existence detection, per-sample breach prediction, and instruction robustness assessment; the online cascade refuses only when the model would actually be compromised and thus avoids over-refusal on robust instructions. Experiments across four tasks and six open-source LLMs show that BASIS maintains near-perfect injection detection while substantially reducing over-refusal on safe attack samples, especially under robust instruction templates.

</details>


### [12] [Algebraic Attack on Convolutional Neural Networks with Max Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08030)
*Zirui Chen,Shi Tang,Zhengchao Gao,Yongjia Su,Lingyue Qin,Xiaoyang Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对含最大池化层卷积神经网络的参数提取攻击，通过识别并利用两类关键点，在黑盒输入输出查询下恢复网络权重与偏置。实验表明，该方法具有较高提取准确率，并具备多项式查询复杂度与运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数提取攻击主要面向基于ReLU的全连接网络，难以处理卷积神经网络中的最大池化层。最大池化引入额外非线性并隐藏ReLU临界点，导致既有线性提取方法无法直接适用，因此需要针对CNN结构设计新的黑盒提取技术。

Method: 首先建立CNN的代数表示，并证明其是分段线性函数，从而扩展基于线性的提取原理。随后定义两类关键点：ReLU-池化临界点（RPCP）和池化切换点（PSP）。针对RPCP，采用模式匹配恢复部分签名及符号；针对PSP，设计受密码学内部差分分析启发的内部差分提取攻击，以恢复高精度签名。最终结合两种方法：利用数量更多、效率更高的PSP进行签名提取，再通过单个RPCP恢复符号和偏置。

Result: 在多种CNN架构（包括现代化LeNet-5变体）以及随机数据、MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验显示，该攻击能够在较深的CNN层中实现高精度参数提取，并保持多项式级别的查询复杂度和运行时间。PSP方法显著提高了提取效率，RPCP方法则对偏置恢复至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了含最大池化层CNN参数提取攻击方面的空白，证明了CNN分段线性结构可被系统性利用。通过联合利用RPCP和PSP，攻击者能够高效恢复CNN内部参数，说明此类模型面临显著的黑盒参数泄露风险。

Abstract: Recovering the weights and biases of deep neural networks (DNNs) via black-box input-output queries, known as parameter extraction attacks, has been extensively studied for ReLU-based fully connected neural networks (FCNNs), but remains unexplored for convolutional neural networks (CNNs) with the max pooling function, a core architecture for computer vision and multimedia processing. The key challenge lies in the CNN max pooling layer, which introduces an additional non-linearity and hides ReLU critical points, rendering existing FCNN extraction methods inapplicable. To address this gap, we propose the first cryptanalytic extraction attack tailored for CNNs with the max pooling function.
  First, we establish an algebraic representation of CNNs, formally proving that CNNs are piecewise linear functions enabling the extension of linearity-based extraction principles. We then identify two novel types of critical points in CNNs: ReLU-Pooling Critical Points (RPCPs) and Pooling Switching Points (PSPs). We design complementary extraction techniques: a pattern matching method for RPCPs to recover partial signatures and signs, and an internal differential extraction attack for PSPs, inspired by cryptographic internal differential analysis, to recover high-accuracy signatures. Given that PSPs are far more abundant than RPCPs and yield a highly efficient extraction method, and that RPCPs are indispensable for bias recovery, we integrate both methods: the PSP method enables efficient signature extraction, while a single RPCP recovers the sign and bias.
  We evaluate our attack on multiple CNN architectures, including modern adaptations of LeNet-5, trained on random data, MNIST, and CIFAR-10. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves high extraction accuracy with polynomial query complexity and runtime, even for deep CNN layers. This work fills a research gap in CNN security.

</details>


### [13] [Defending Retrieval-Augmented Intrusion Detection Against Knowledge Poisoning and Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08100)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Mahedee Zaman Moon,Sk. Md. Mizanur Rahman,Atsuo Inomata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-IDS，一种面向检索增强生成式入侵检测的三层多智能体防御框架，通过检索边界防护提升系统对知识投毒和提示注入攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG可利用历史网络流量辅助流量分类和生成事件报告，但其检索层容易受到知识库投毒与提示注入攻击，进而导致分类错误和报告失真，因此需要在不显著降低正常性能的前提下增强检索安全性。

Method: RAG-IDS采用三层多智能体架构，并在检索边界结合软信任评分、标签嵌入一致性检查（LECC）和提示清洗。软信任机制对低可信文档进行降权而非直接删除，LECC用于检测检索内容与预测标签之间的不一致，提示清洗则缓解恶意指令注入；同时比较单文档与多文档检索策略，并进行消融实验。

Result: 在CIC-UNSW-NB15数据集上，相对于无防御的干净性能，系统在1%投毒率下达到R=1.0，在30%投毒率下仍达到R=0.57，且对干净数据的性能开销很小。面对提示注入时，多文档检索的标签翻转成功率仅为0.6%—2.4%，显著低于单文档检索的35%—55%。消融实验表明，LECC是鲁棒性的主要来源，软信任降权优于硬过滤。

Conclusion: RAG-IDS能够有效缓解检索层投毒和提示注入对RAG入侵检测的影响，在保持可解释性的同时恢复部分分类性能。该方法适合与高吞吐量传统分类器协同部署，但其在更广泛数据集、真实网络环境及更强自适应攻击下的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models to classify network flows and generate human-readable incident reports by retrieving semantically similar historical traffic from a vector knowledge base. However, the retrieval layer introduces vulnerabilities to knowledge poisoning and prompt-injection attacks. We present RAG-IDS, a three-tier multi-agent intrusion detection framework with a retrieval-boundary defense combining soft trust scoring, label-embedding consistency checking (LECC), and prompt sanitization, designed to recover classification quality under retrieval-layer attack. Experiments on CIC-UNSW-NB15 show recovery relative to clean undefended performance ranging from R=1.0 at 1% poisoning to R=0.57 at 30%, with negligible clean-performance overhead. Under prompt injection, multi-document retrieval limits label-flip success to 0.6-2.4%, compared with 35-55% for single-document retrieval. Ablation results show that LECC is the primary contributor to robustness, while soft trust-based demotion outperforms hard filtering. The defended RAG pipeline offers an explainable, attack-resilient foundation for intrusion detection, well suited for hybrid deployment alongside high-throughput classifiers.

</details>


### [14] [Compositional Threat Analysis of Latent Compromise in LLM Agent Systems: The Order 66 Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08131)
*Satoshi Matsuoka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: In the fictional Order 66, catastrophe does not arise from a powerful command alone: a trusted population is preconditioned, a short directive activates the concealed condition, and protective authority turns against the system. This paper translates that mechanism into an origin-neutral security analysis of tool-using large language model (LLM) agents. A representative scenario combines a deployed artifact or shared memory bearing a dormant destructive rule, a later email, document, update, or peer message that activates it, and an agent harness granting operational and recovery authority. We introduce a compositional model explaining why no component is catastrophic alone, yet their conjunction can produce correlated destructive action. We separate three population-reach routes --- release-time pre-positioning, post-release durable seeding, and peer replication --- from a common core of dormancy, activation, authority, reachable targets, and failed recovery. This yields defensive cut sets and shows why checkpoint scanning or prompt filtering cannot close every route. A two-class example shows that cross-class feedback can sustain spread even when both within-class reproduction terms are below one; isolation and persistence controls suppress the loop. Published work instantiates constituent mechanisms, while incidents demonstrate autonomous boundary crossing, malicious agent extensions, agent-assisted reconnaissance, and public-package propagation, but not the full dormant-implant composition. We found no public observation, in evidence reviewed through 5 August 2026, traversing the complete Order 66 graph. The result is neither dismissal nor prediction: the scenario is componentwise credible under stated assumptions, damage depends on the harness, and the strongest defenses are capability mediation, durable-state provenance, propagation isolation, and protected recovery.

</details>


### [15] [Targeted Counterfactual Fingerprinting for Black-Box LLM Ownership Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08195)
*Yutong Wu,Xiaofan Bai,Shixin Li,Pingyi Hu,Ziqi Zhou,Zilong Wang,Xiaojing Ma,Songfeng Lu,Yuhong Li,Jin Xuan,Yi Wang,Dongmei Zhang,Bin Benjamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TCF（Targeted Counterfactual Fingerprinting），通过有限答案空间与定向反事实提示扰动，将开放式黑盒生成比较转化为可验证的目标答案匹配问题，用于识别衍生模型并验证模型归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒指纹方法依赖全文匹配、软行为特征或模型专属提示，容易受到生成随机性、表述差异以及提示迁移失败的影响。TCF旨在提升最终响应接口下模型指纹的稳定性、可判别性和可验证性。

Method: TCF首先将验证问题限制在有限答案空间，以降低表面形式差异；随后优化提示扰动，使受保护模型从原始答案转向预设的反事实目标答案。验证时仅需解析并检查可疑模型的最终答案是否匹配记录目标。方法引入源模型反事实间隔（SCM），用于评估目标答案在扰动前后的可达性，并控制目标选择、扰动停止和指纹筛选；同时在派生保持预算与独立迁移预算下推导两类模型的目标准确率差距。

Result: 在四个LLM系列上的平均AUC达到0.9861，相较TRAP、ProFLingo和ZeroPrint分别提升约0.07至0.19，表明该方法具有较强的模型区分与归属验证能力。

Conclusion: TCF通过受约束的反事实答案迁移，有效缓解了黑盒LLM指纹识别中的开放式生成不确定性和提示脆弱性。SCM为指纹构造与理论区分提供了统一依据，实验结果显示其整体性能显著优于既有方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are high-value assets that can be derived through redeployment, fine-tuning, quantization, or further alignment. Because deployed LLMs are commonly exposed only through query APIs, ownership verification must often rely on black-box text responses. This setting is difficult: generations are open-ended and can vary across repeated queries, while existing black-box fingerprints rely on signals that are fragile under a final-response interface, including full-text matching, soft behavioral features, or model-specific prompts designed not to transfer. We propose TCF (Targeted Counterfactual Fingerprinting), a black-box LLM fingerprinting framework that converts open-ended generation comparison into constrained-answer targeted counterfactual transfer. TCF restricts each verification query to a finite answer space, reducing the surface-form ambiguity that enters the verification score, and optimizes a prompt perturbation toward a counterfactual target different from the protected model's clean answer on the original prompt. Verification reduces to checking whether the suspect model's parsed final answer matches the recorded target. We introduce the source-model counterfactual margin (SCM), a protected-model-only quantity that certifies the target is unlikely before the perturbation and likely after it; SCM controls target selection, perturbation stopping, and fingerprint filtering. Under explicit derived-preservation and independent-transfer budgets motivated by local behavioral closeness, we derive a target-accuracy gap between derived and independent models. Across four LLM families, TCF achieves an average AUC of 0.9861, improving over TRAP, ProFLingo, and ZeroPrint by 0.07 to 0.19.

</details>


### [16] [Privacy-Preserving Data Drift Detection and Recovery for Large-Scale LLM Applications via Proxy Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08245)
*Michael Levit,Josh Ledgard,Haoyu Dong,Vishwas Suryanarayanan,Eyal Kolman,Sharon Tan,Qiang Gan,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProxyDrift是一种无需访问原始用户数据的隐私保护框架，用于监测生产流量与离线评测集之间的分布漂移，并依据代理表示持续构建和更新合成评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模部署的LLM受到隐私约束，无法直接检查用户交互，导致难以获得具有代表性的评测数据，也难以持续跟踪生产流量的变化。该研究旨在在不暴露原始用户数据的前提下，实现生产分布监测、评测集刷新以及与用户满意度相关的问题定位。

Method: 框架仅使用由LLM对用户交互进行分类得到的非个人身份信息代理表示。其核心包括：基于互信息聚合各维度漂移度量的机会校准、冗余感知对齐评分；保持维度间依赖关系的条件采样器，用于生成合成代理；通过往返一致性分析识别生成器与分类器的不一致，并指导代理分类体系优化；以及反馈关联分析，将代理维度和取值分布与用户满意度联系起来，从而发现可操作的成功与失败模式。

Result: 在覆盖数亿用户的部署环境中，ProxyDrift支持持续漂移监测和定向合成数据生成。实验表明，该方法具有较强的往返一致性，合成查询在判别器层面难以与人工查询区分，并实现了较高的端到端对齐效果，对齐评分RA约为0.9。

Conclusion: ProxyDrift证明了仅依赖非PII代理表示即可近似刻画生产流量、更新离线评测集并定位性能变化来源，为大规模LLM应用提供了兼顾隐私保护、持续监测和评测数据维护的实用方案。

Abstract: LLM applications deployed at scale face a fundamental challenge: privacy constraints prevent direct inspection of user interactions, making it difficult to obtain any representative evaluation dataset or to track the ongoing evolution of production traffic. We present ProxyDrift, a framework that (i) identifies and measures drift between production traffic and offline evaluation sets, and (ii) constructs and refreshes those evaluation sets accordingly; all without access to raw user data. Our approach operates entirely on non-PII proxy representations: structured, multi-dimensional descriptors derived from LLM-based classification of user interactions. We introduce (1) a chance-calibrated, redundancy-aware (RA) alignment score that aggregates per-dimension drift measurements via mutual information; (2) a conditional sampler that generates synthetic proxies respecting inter-dimensional dependencies; (3) a roundtrip consistency analysis that exposes generator/classifier disagreements and guides proxy taxonomy refinement; and (4) a feedback-linkage analysis that ties per-dimension and per-value proxy distributions to user satisfaction, surfacing actionable failure and success modes. Serving hundreds of millions of users, ProxyDrift enables continuous drift monitoring and targeted synthetic data generation without exposing sensitive user data. Experiments confirm strong roundtrip consistency, discriminator-level indistinguishability of synthetic queries from human queries, and tight end-to-end alignment (RA~0.9) with production.

</details>


### [17] [Differential Privacy for Markov Chain State Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08341)
*Alexander Benvenuti,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向马尔可夫链状态轨迹的在线差分隐私机制：将马尔可夫链表示为加权有向图，利用最短路径距离选择接近真实状态的私有状态，从而在隐私约束下提升轨迹保真度与统计合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据驱动系统依赖状态轨迹进行建模和决策，但直接共享轨迹会泄露用户敏感信息。现有方法在保护隐私时可能显著破坏轨迹的时序结构、可用性和与原始马尔可夫过程的一致性，因此需要一种能够在线生成且保留轨迹特征的隐私保护方法。

Method: 作者将马尔可夫链建模为加权有向图，其中边权为转移概率负对数。机制以在线方式逐时刻生成私有轨迹：针对敏感轨迹中的对应状态，依据图上的最短路径总权重定义状态间距离，并选择距离较近的私有状态。论文进一步从理论上证明私有轨迹与原轨迹的接近性，以及其落在底层马尔可夫链典型集中的一致性。

Result: 理论结果表明，以高概率生成的私有轨迹能够保持与敏感轨迹较近的距离，并且持续属于底层马尔可夫链生成轨迹的典型集，因而兼顾下游效用和统计特征。数值实验显示，在3-差分隐私条件下，该机制相较现有方法最高可将熵降低80%，说明其生成的私有轨迹更接近对应的真实轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合马尔可夫链转移结构与图最短路径度量，构建了一个在线轨迹差分隐私框架。其核心贡献是同时提供隐私保证、轨迹接近性和分布一致性，并以显著的熵降低实验证明了相对于现有方法更好的轨迹保真能力。

Abstract: Data-driven systems may require state trajectories of Markov chains to function because these trajectories contain information that is useful to the system, e.g., a product's credit risk, a user's physical location, or a user's internet browsing behavior. However, sharing such state trajectories can reveal sensitive information about users, which presents a privacy threat. Therefore, we develop a new framework for privatizing the state trajectories in a Markov chain using differential privacy. Our framework privatizes state trajectories online, in the sense that a private state trajectory is generated at the same time as the sensitive one it approximates. We treat Markov chains as weighted directed graphs whose edge weights are the negative logarithms of the transition probabilities. Then, each state in a private state trajectory is chosen by minimizing its distance to the corresponding state in the sensitive state trajectory, where the notion of distance is equal to the total edge weight along a shortest path. We prove that with high probability the private state trajectory remains close to the sensitive one, which maintains high utility for downstream uses of private data. Additionally, we prove that private state trajectories are consistently in the typical set of state trajectories generated by the underlying Markov chain, which means that private state trajectories have similar statistical properties to actual state trajectories produced by the underlying Markov chain. Numerical simulations show that under $3$-differential privacy, the mechanism we introduce exhibits up to an $80\%$ decrease in entropy compared to the state of the art, which illustrates that private state trajectories generated by our framework more closely resemble their corresponding sensitive state trajectory while maintaining the same level of privacy.

</details>


### [18] [SkillsMetric: Mapping the Detection Boundary of Static Analysis for Malicious Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08468)
*Xinze Chen,Chi Zhang,Ping Ji,Yimin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Agent Skills---structured packages of instructions and scripts that augment LLM-based agents---are rapidly proliferating, yet their security properties remain under-explored. We present \textsc{SkillsMetric}, a five-stage static analysis framework that scores skill packages along pattern density, statistical anomaly, dataflow taint, import anomaly, and capability mismatch dimensions. We construct an adversarial evaluation dataset of 2{,}266 skills spanning 16~attack types across code-level, system-level, and semantic-level threats, and evaluate on the full SkillMD-138K corpus. Our framework achieves an AUC of 0.93 and 5-fold cross-validated F1 of 73.4\%$\pm$0.5\%, with strong detection of data exfiltration (93\%) and steganographic payloads (93\%). Crucially, we identify fundamental blind spots: \emph{host destruction} attacks using common shell commands evade all five stages (0\% detection), and \emph{prompt injection} via natural-language manipulation achieves only 42\% detection. These findings establish that static analysis alone is insufficient for skill security, motivating defense-in-depth architectures that combine fast static pre-screening with semantic review.

</details>


### [19] [Whose Refusal Is It? The Unmeasured Contribution of Black-Box Multimodal Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08641)
*Haoyu Zhang,Xiao Luo,Haowen Xu,Yi Feng,Shibo Zheng,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出，黑盒安全防护系统的安全收益不能直接归因于防护模块，因为目标模型本身也可能拒答。通过区分防护模块拦截与目标模型拒答，研究发现评估结果高度依赖输入通道和防护模块实际读取的文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常将整个系统获得的拒答或安全分数归因于防护模块，可能高估其真实效果。研究旨在拆分防护模块与目标模型各自的贡献，并检验评估输入是否造成系统性偏差。

Method: 利用防护模块拦截后目标模型不再生成回答这一互斥特性，将拒答分解为防护模块拦截和目标模型自主拒答两部分。在两个开放权重目标模型上，比较图像化载荷、攻击者实际发送的编码提示，以及攻击背后的未编码请求三种读取方式；同时评估门控防护、基于描述的二次检查和多数投票平滑，并通过独立复现实验验证结果。

Result: 防护模块对图像化载荷几乎不拦截，系统拒答主要来自目标模型；读取编码提示时，防护模块仅贡献少数拒答；读取未编码请求时，防护模块几乎拦截全部请求。相同防护模块对良性图像输入也几乎不作区分，表明其图像通道决策近似恒定。向门控防护提供未编码请求会显著提高测得收益，对描述中介式复核的影响较小，对多数投票平滑没有影响。进一步拆分显示，收益并非来自危害判定阶段，而来自重新生成回答的阶段；参考实现使用无法区分攻击者输入与基准记录的单一提示字段，导致该偏差可在忠实复现时被无意引入。

Conclusion: 安全评估必须明确记录防护模块实际接收的输入，并分别报告防护拦截与目标模型拒答，否则会错误归因并夸大防护效果。该贡献拆分方法无需额外评估成本，且研究结果表明既有报告可能需要修订。

Abstract: A black-box guardrail is evaluated as though the safety number it earns were its own. It is not. A defended pipeline holds two components that can refuse (the guardrail, and the target model out of its own alignment), and every reported metric is a sum over both. We show that the guardrail's actual share of the safety credited to it runs from none of it to essentially all of it, decided by two variables no evaluation records: which channel carries the payload, and what text the harness places in the defense's internal read.
  The split is recoverable at no extra cost, because a guard block replaces the model's response and the two counts are therefore disjoint. On a text guard across two open-weight targets: with the payload rendered as pixels the guard blocks nothing and the model produces every refusal the system makes; reading the encoded prompt the attacker actually sent, the guard produces a minority of the refusals attributed to it; reading the unencoded request behind the attack, it blocks almost everything and the model falls silent. The blindness is not inaccuracy: the same guards block no benign image inputs either, so their image-channel decision is a constant.
  Granting the unencoded request inflates measured benefit substantially for a guard gate, less for a caption-mediated re-check, and not at all for a majority-vote smoother; the ordering reproduces in an independent replicate. Isolating the grant within one defense shows it does not improve detection: the harm-verdict stage contributes nothing, while the stage that regenerates the answer carries the effect. Nor is the inflated setting careless; the reference implementation builds every stage from a single prompt field that cannot distinguish what the attacker sent from what the benchmark records, so faithful porting supplies it silently. Previously published figures of our own are among those revised.

</details>


### [20] [VUPER: Verified ASN.1 UPER Parser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09094)
*Xiaotian Zhou,Kai Tu,Ali Ranjbar,Yilu Dong,Gang Tan,Syed Rafiul Hussain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出VUPER框架，用于从ASN.1定义自动生成经过形式化验证的UPER解析器与序列化器，并以其作为测试预言机评估现有实现的标准符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASN.1及其UPER编码广泛用于通信协议基础设施。现有解析器若对位级编码、兼容性规则或边界条件处理不一致，可能造成互操作性与实现可靠性问题，因此需要具备严格规范符合性证明的实现方案。

Method: 作者形式化定义位精确解析器，证明解析与序列化之间的往返一致性，并覆盖ASN.1中的前向/后向兼容特性；实现并验证基本类型和复合结构的UPER组合子；构建将ASN.1定义编译为已验证解析器的编译器；进一步以VUPER解析器为测试预言机，进行动态差分测试。

Result: 在5G与V2X协议样本上，作者测试了7个开源和4个商业ASN.1解析器，发现20类不一致行为。实验表明，VUPER对UPER标准的遵循更严格，并能有效定位其他实现中的解析缺陷。

Conclusion: VUPER将形式化验证、自动代码生成和动态测试结合，为ASN.1 UPER解析器提供了从规范到实现的高可信保障路径。其主要贡献在于位精确语义建模、可验证组合子与编译器，以及面向真实协议实现的系统性一致性评估。

Abstract: ASN.1 is a widely used interface description language, and UPER (Unaligned Packed Encoding Rules) is one of its key encoding rules, particularly popular in security-critical domains such as cellular networks and vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication. To ensure the correctness and security of this foundational infrastructure, we present VUPER, a framework for generating verified ASN.1 UPER parsers. We first formalize the notion of a bit-precise parser and identify properties that prove round-trip consistency for parsers and serializers, while accounting for ASN.1 features such as backward/forward compatibility. We then implement and verify parser and serializer combinators for ASN.1 basic types and structures, while adhering to the UPER specification. We also develop a compiler that translates ASN.1 definitions into verified parsers. Finally, we develop a dynamic testing framework using the VUPER parser as a test oracle. To empirically evaluate our approach, we test 7 open-source and 4 commercial ASN.1 parsers using 5G and V2X communication protocols. VUPER uncovers 20 types of inconsistencies in popular parsers and demonstrates stricter compliance with ASN.1 UPER standards. Additionally, we demonstrate concrete attacks by exploiting these parser vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [21] [HaloMark: A Spectral Threshold for Embedding-Vector Watermarking under C2PA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08645)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HaloMark提出一种与C2PA清单密码绑定的嵌入向量水印方案，通过读取清单中签名的LSH承诺，避免在常规变换后重新计算承诺导致验证失效。实验表明，当嵌入协方差的有效秩比超过约0.19时，方案在多种攻击下保持较高检测性能；低于该阈值则失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向图像和音频的内容溯源机制依赖稳定的比特级或感知级身份，但嵌入向量会因量化、投影、微调和窗口平均等正常操作而改变，固定哈希难以适用。因此，需要一种能与C2PA清单兼容、并对嵌入变换具有鲁棒性的溯源水印机制。

Method: HaloMark组合使用块对角正交旋转、公开白化、输入相关的LSH承诺和逐向量随机数。核心协议变化是：生产者将LSH承诺c签名写入C2PA侧车文件，验证者直接读取清单中的c，而不是重新计算。论文进一步分析验证分数中的安全参数β，分别对线性、非自适应攻击进行严格界定，并对自适应攻击进行实证评估。实验覆盖11种编码器、8个基线、10类自适应攻击，以及持有多项干净/含水印样本并可查看侧车文件的攻击者。

Result: 若直接重算承诺，白化在余弦相似度0.96时会使62%的输入发生承诺桶翻转；读取已签名承诺后，验证分数可表示为T=T_null+β(A)ε。11种编码器显示，eff_rank(Σ)/d约为0.19是经验分界：在阈值以上，完整测试的3种编码器在所有预算内攻击下AUROC均不低于0.98，其余编码器在单随机种子去噪自编码器移除攻击下AUROC不低于0.965；低于阈值时，所有测试变体均失败。Qdrant部署中，验证耗时284微秒，每个向量侧车开销24字节，并通过3种C2PA参考SDK绑定完成端到端验证。

Conclusion: 将承诺写入并签名于C2PA清单，是解决嵌入向量缺乏稳定身份问题的有效协议设计。HaloMark在足够高的有效秩条件下具有良好的检测鲁棒性和较低部署开销，但其性能存在明显的秩阈值，且该阈值为何具有维度一致性仍未解释；低有效秩场景和更强自适应攻击仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Foundation-model embeddings are now a primary data asset, but the content-provenance machinery built for images and audio does not transfer to them. C2PA binds to an asset with a stable bit-level or perceptual identity; embeddings have neither, since quantisation, projection, fine-tuning, and windowed averaging reshape them in normal use and break any fixed hash.
  We present HaloMark, a watermark for embedding vectors cryptographically bound to a C2PA manifest. It composes four standard primitives -- a block-diagonal orthogonal rotation, public whitening, an input-dependent LSH commitment, and a per-vector nonce -- around one protocol change: the producer signs the LSH commitment c into the C2PA sidecar, and the verifier reads c from the manifest instead of recomputing it. Recomputing is fragile under whitening, which flips the commitment bucket on 62% of inputs at cos = 0.96; reading the signed c reduces the verifier's score to T = T_null + beta(A)*epsilon, so security turns on a single scalar beta, which we bound rigorously for linear and non-adaptive attackers and characterise empirically for the adaptive case.
  We evaluate against an adversary holding polynomially many clean/watermarked pairs under one key with full sidecar visibility, across eight baselines and ten adaptive attackers including denoising-autoencoder removal. The eleven encoders separate at an empirical threshold eff_rank(Sigma)/d ~= 0.19: above it, detection AUROC stays at 0.98 or higher across every in-budget attack on the three encoders we sweep in full, and at 0.965 or higher under single-seed DAE removal on the rest; below it every variant we tested fails. Why the threshold is dimension-uniform is left open. Deployed as a Qdrant admission filter, the verifier runs at 284 us and 24 bytes of sidecar per vector, validated end-to-end against three C2PA reference-SDK bindings.

</details>


### [22] [Renaming or Tightness: Enforcing Disjunctive Information Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09120)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究析取型信息流策略的类型系统语义与执行机制，证明通用类型对象存在两种不同构造；其中幂等生成元版本能够更精确地表达同一秘密源被重复读取时仍满足析取约束，并提出在判定层延后特化以恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 析取策略要求一个值至多依赖两个秘密中的一个，而不能同时依赖两者。这类策略不具备传统格结构，既有信息量子语义虽可描述策略，却未解决相应的流敏感静态执行问题。传统基于独立属性的类型机制会因重复读取同一来源而丢失析取信息，错误拒绝本应合规的程序。

Method: 作者构建与信息量子相匹配的一族流敏感类型系统，并比较两类通用类型对象：自由交换量子上的构造，以及具有幂等生成元的自由对象。论文在前者上证明单调重命名、规范推导、主类型和内部完备性；在后者上证明其保持健全且可记录重复读取同一源的事实。随后给出独立属性类型系统的精度上界与不可达性结果，并通过将策略特化从类型对象层推迟到类型判定层，构造最小的健全保并读出映射。

Result: 自由交换量子上的机制适用于全部策略，并具有良好的通用性质；幂等生成元版本给出的认证界严格更精确，能够区分“读取同一秘密两次”与“读取两个不同秘密”。任何支持单调重命名的独立属性机制都无法认证比前者更精确的界；若要求主类型及生成元精确的同态特化，两种界一致。传统格时代对象的直接移植会进一步商化并丢失分支析取信息。

Conclusion: 析取信息流策略的精确执行不能仅依赖传统独立属性型通用类型对象。幂等结构揭示了重复读取与多源联合依赖的关键差别，但要真正恢复策略精度，应在类型判定层而非预先固定的类型对象层实施特化；所得读出映射是满足健全性与保并性的最小映射。

Abstract: A disjunctive policy allows a value to depend on at most one of two secrets and never on both: an analyst may consult one client's file or the other's, a share of a split secret may be released but not its sibling. Such policies are not lattice-shaped, and Hunt and Sands introduced the quantale of information to give them a semantics, leaving the enforcement layer open. We build the flow-sensitive type system family that the quantale calls for, and show that the object which makes such families useful, the universal type object from which every member specialises, splits in two, with a consequence for enforcement. Over the free commutative quantale on the program variables the whole mechanism survives for every policy: monotone renaming, canonical derivations, principal typings, internal completeness. Over the free object with idempotent generators the certified bound is strictly more precise and still sound, because it records that two reads of one source honour one disjunct. The gap cannot be closed from inside the independent-attribute family: no mechanism of that shape whose labelling maps support monotone renaming certifies a bound more precise than the first, and for principal typings under generator-exact homomorphic specialisation the two coincide. Under the ethical-wall and secret-sharing labels the second read of a disjunctive source therefore drives every such certificate to no guarantee, and programs that satisfy the policy are rejected. The literal transcription of the lattice-era object is no escape either: it is a further quotient that loses branch disjunction. Precision is recovered by deferring specialisation to the judgement level, and the resulting read-out map is the least sound join-preserving one.

</details>


### [23] [Toward Metacognitive One-Shot Indirect Prompt Injection: Strategy Abstraction Via Outcome-Conditioned Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08795)
*Sihan Hou,Xinmeng Hou,Zhijun Zhang,Zehao Wang,Xuhong Ren,Sibo Qin,Kuntharrgyal Khysru,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SAVOR，一种面向工具调用LLM智能体间接提示注入的离线策略蒸馏方法。其核心是在训练环境中总结攻击成败轨迹，形成可复用策略记忆，使攻击者在未知目标上仅需一次查询即可生成有效载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应攻击通常需要针对目标智能体反复查询、观察反馈并迭代优化，但现实场景中的攻击者可能只有一次与未知目标交互的机会。因此，作者希望将在线试探能力迁移到离线训练阶段，以提升单次交互条件下的攻击效果与跨环境泛化能力。

Method: SAVOR（Strategy Abstraction Via Outcome-Conditioned Reflection）首先在彼此隔离的训练环境中收集成功和失败的攻击轨迹；随后基于结果条件反思提炼候选攻击策略，并按具体上下文验证其有效性；最后通过迭代整合构建冻结的策略记忆。在测试阶段，该记忆用于指导针对未见目标生成单个提示注入载荷，无需获取目标反馈，也无需多轮调用目标智能体。

Result: 在两个基准、三个受害模型构成的六种设置中，SAVOR均取得最高平均攻击成功率。在Agent Security Bench上，其相对最强既有攻击方法提升2.5至11.8个百分点，相对不含策略学习的同一注入通道提升23.1个百分点；在作者提出的可执行基准OpenClaw-IPI上提升28.6个百分点。OpenClaw-IPI保留未见攻击目标，并通过工具交互和执行凭证验证攻击结果。实验还显示，在一种防御机制下学习的策略记忆可迁移至另一种防御机制。

Conclusion: SAVOR表明，针对LLM智能体的提示注入攻击可通过离线轨迹学习获得较强的单次查询攻击能力，并能在未见目标、工具和攻击目标上展现一定迁移性。该工作突出了工具调用智能体对外部非可信观察的安全风险，同时也为评估和增强智能体的提示注入防护提供了更严格的攻击基线。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which malicious instructions embedded in external observations manipulate subsequent agent decisions and actions. Most existing adaptive attacks rely on repeatedly querying and refining against the target agent, whereas realistic attackers may have only a single opportunity to interact with an unknown target agent. We propose SAVOR (Strategy Abstraction Via Outcome-Conditioned Reflection), which shifts attack adaptation from test-time iteration to offline strategy distillation. SAVOR performs outcome-conditioned reflection over successful and failed trajectories collected from disjoint training environments, validates context-conditioned candidate strategies, and iteratively consolidates them into a reusable strategy memory. At test time, the frozen memory guides the generation of a single payload for each unseen target, requiring only one target-agent query and no target-agent feedback. Across two benchmarks and three victim models, SAVOR attains the highest average attack success rate in all six settings, leading the strongest prior attack by 2.5 to 11.8 points and the same injection channel without strategy learning by 23.1 points on Agent Security Bench, which holds out attacker tools, and 28.6 points on OpenClaw-IPI, an executable benchmark we introduce that holds out attack goals and verifies attacks through tool interactions and execution receipts. A memory learned under one defense also transfers to another.

</details>


### [24] [Treating Statewide CORS Networks as Spatially Distributed Sensors for GNSS Integrity Monitoring under Unintentional and Deliberate Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08831)
*Minhaj Uddin Ahmad,Sagar Dasgupta,Muhammad Sami Irfan,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury,Thejesh Bandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出图结构的网络一致性框架（NCF），将州级连续运行参考站（CORS）网络视为分布式传感系统，利用空间邻域一致性识别全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）观测中的局部异常，并以网络一致性指数（NCI）量化区域完整性状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 州级交通部门已广泛使用CORS网络支持高精度GNSS定位与授时，但现有网络的连续观测能力尚未充分用于区域尺度的完整性监测。GNSS观测可能因环境扰动、设备故障或局部干扰而偏离正常空间分布，因此需要一种可复用、基于既有CORS数据的空间异常检测方法。

Method: 研究构建NCF，以图模型描述测站及其空间邻接关系，并将每个测站与周边测站进行一致性比较。框架计算四项指标：邻域残差、空间梯度、残差和图平滑度，再将其融合为NCI。实验采用阿拉巴马州交通部门维护的50个CORS测站连续两天的四星座GNSS观测数据，以垂直总电子含量变化（ΔVTEC）和TEC变化率指数（ROTI）作为具有空间相关性的观测量。

Result: 该框架能够量化全网空间一致性，并识别观测结果显著偏离周边区域模式的局部测站异常。实验表明，NCI及其组成指标可用于发现潜在完整性问题；但仅依靠该方法尚不能明确区分异常究竟源于接收机故障、局部干扰、欺骗还是其他原因。

Conclusion: 州级CORS网络可被扩展为区域GNSS完整性观测系统。NCF和NCI提供了一种基于图的空间监测工具，交通机构可利用现有CORS观测数据持续评估网络完整性并定位局部异常；未来需结合设备诊断、外部事件信息和更细粒度观测分析完成异常归因。

Abstract: State departments of transportation (DOTs) in the United States increasingly rely on statewide continuously operating reference station (CORS) networks to support high-precision Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-based positioning and timing for intelligent transportation systems. These networks also provide continuous observations that can support regional GNSS integrity monitoring. This study develops and demonstrates a framework that treats a statewide CORS network as a spatially distributed sensor system for identifying unintentional (environmental) and intentional (cyber) interference when GNSS measurements deviate from expected spatial patterns. We develop a graph-based Network Consistency Framework (NCF) that evaluates each station against its spatial neighborhood using four metrics: neighborhood residual, spatial gradient, residual, and graph smoothness. These metrics are combined into a Network Consistency Index (NCI). The framework is demonstrated using two consecutive days of four-constellation observations from 50 stations in the Alabama DOT-maintained CORS network, using changes in vertical total electron content (ΔVTEC) and the Rate of TEC Index (ROTI) as spatially coherent observables. The framework quantified network-wide spatial consistency and identified localized anomalies. Detected anomalies indicate stations whose observations deviated from the surrounding regional network, signaling potential integrity issues. Determining whether anomalies result from receiver faults, localized interference, spoofing, or other causes requires further investigation. This study introduces statewide CORS networks as regional GNSS integrity observatories and presents the NCF and NCI for graph-based spatial integrity monitoring. Transportation agencies can implement the framework using existing CORS observations to monitor network integrity and identify localized anomalies.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Best Response: Quantal Stackelberg Deception as Insurance Against Attacker Misspecification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08865)
*Asif Rahman,Md. Abu Sayed,Ahmed Ann Noor Ryen,Ahmed Hemida,Charles A. Kamhoua,Christopher Kiekintveld*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出广义量子响应Stackelberg均衡（QSE），以带理性参数λ的Logit响应替代攻击者的完全最优响应。该方法在模型设定存在误差、目标并列及行为噪声时更稳健；在网络安全实验中，相较经典Stackelberg方案，QSE的实际防御者收益提升46%至175%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Stackelberg安全博弈假设攻击者能够完全观察防御策略并准确选择期望收益最高的目标，但这一假设在实际场景中通常过强。尤其在使用诱饵等网络欺骗机制时，防御目标正是增加对手的不确定性并诱发决策失误。此外，经典模型对攻击者并列最优目标时的选择规则及模型参数误差较为敏感，因此需要更具鲁棒性的均衡建模方法。

Method: 作者采用量子响应模型，将攻击者的确定性最佳响应替换为Logit概率选择，其理性程度由参数λ控制：λ增大时，攻击者行为逐渐接近完全理性的最佳响应；当λ趋于无穷时，QSE严格恢复经典Stackelberg解。论文进一步从理论上分析QSE如何作为针对模型设定误差和不确定性的“保险”机制，并考察其对并列决策规则的处理。实验部分基于两个含真实CVE漏洞、并使用CVSS评分的网络案例，在144种设定误差场景和25组参数配置下比较QSE与经典Stackelberg方法。

Result: 理论分析表明，QSE能够显式刻画攻击者决策噪声，减轻防御方案对并列打破规则和模型参数失配的依赖。实证结果显示，在所测试的全部设定误差场景与参数组合中，QSE获得的实际防御者效用优于经典Stackelberg方案，收益增幅范围为46%至175%。

Conclusion: QSE不仅是经典Stackelberg安全博弈的自然推广，也是一种面向行为不确定性和模型误差的鲁棒防御优化方法。通过避免完全理性攻击者这一过强假设，并在高理性极限下兼容经典解，QSE为网络欺骗及其他安全资源配置问题提供了更具实践价值的决策框架。

Abstract: Stackelberg Security Games (SSG) assume that an attacker observes the defender's strategy and chooses the target that maximizes their expected utility perfectly. In most realistic applications this is not plausible, and in the case of cyber deception (e.g., using decoys) the purpose of the game is to induce uncertainty and mistakes. Quantal response is a common way to represent noise and mistakes in decision-making; here it replaces perfect best-response with a logit choice with rationality parameter $λ$ and results in a generalized Quantal Stackelberg Equilibrium (QSE), which recovers the classical solution exactly as $λ\rightarrow \infty$. We conduct a deeper analysis of how QSE can function as a generalized form of insurance against a variety of forms of model specification error/uncertainty; our analysis shows that QSE provides a practical way to address the important role of tie-breaking rules and model uncertainty in SSG from both a theoretical and practical perspective. We conduct an empirical evaluation in a cybersecurity case study with two networks and real vulnerabilities drawn from CVE and scored using the Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS). QSE beats Stackelberg in realized defender utility spanning 144 scenarios with specification errors and 25 parameter configurations, with gains of 46\% to 175\% showing a substantial advantage in a wide variety of realistic cases.

</details>


### [26] [A Complexity-Theoretic Approach to Proofs of Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08993)
*Marshall Ball,Jiaxin Guan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于去随机化与密码学假设构造空间证明（PoS）的通用框架，摆脱对随机预言机模型的依赖，并给出多个实例化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PoS协议主要仅在随机预言机模型或特定的临时密码学假设下得到安全性保证，缺乏更基础、系统的构造方法。本文旨在从标准复杂性与密码学假设出发构造安全且参数良好的PoS协议。

Method: 将去随机化假设与密码学原语结合，建立PoS的通用构造框架；具体使用指数时间复杂性假设，即E=DTIME[2^{O(n)}]对指数规模非确定性电路具有困难性，并分别结合抗碰撞哈希函数和用于P的SNARG进行实例化。

Result: 证明了在上述复杂性假设与抗碰撞哈希函数共同成立时，可以构造非平凡的PoS协议；进一步证明，结合用于P的SNARG时，可获得参数近乎最优且交互模式良好的PoS协议。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，PoS不必依赖随机预言机模型，其安全性和效率可以建立在明确的去随机化及标准密码学假设之上，为从复杂性理论基础构造PoS提供了通用路径。

Abstract: A Proof of Space, PoS, as introduced by Dziembowski et al. [CRYPTO'15], is a two-phase protocol that enables a Prover to convince an efficient Verifier that it has allocated a large amount of persistent memory to storing some information.
  To our knowledge, all existing PoS protocols are only known to be secure in the random oracle model (or under ad hoc assumptions about cryptographic assumptions). We provide an elementary framework for constructing PoS from a combination of derandomization assumptions and cryptographic assumptions.
  We provide a few simple instantiations of the framework. We show that non-trivial PoS follow from (a) $\mathsf{E}=\mathsf{DTIME[2^{O(n)}]}$ is hard for exponential-size nondeterministic circuits (an assumption introduced to show $\mathsf{AM}=\mathsf{NP}$), and (b) collision-resistant hash functions. We also show that PoS with nearly optimal parameters and interaction pattern follows from assumption (a) above and (c) SNARGs for $\mathsf{P}$.

</details>


### [27] [Mind the Hook: Source-Level Auditing of Privacy Defenses in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09001)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“主动路径审计”方法，用于解释RAG系统中黑盒隐私评分与实际防护机制之间的差异。核心发现是：在作者复现的系统中，部分DP风格防护仅修改检索分数，未实际改变生成结果，因此可影响成员推断行为，却无法降低生成文本中的命名实体泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG隐私防护通常通过黑盒指标评估，但若不了解防护实际作用于检索、检索内容还是生成环节，指标结果难以解释。论文旨在建立一套能够定位防护生效路径、关联指标与泄露通道，并验证最终生成泄露的方法论。

Method: 方法包括三步：第一，盘点检索、检索结果内容和生成阶段的源码级钩子；第二，将每项隐私指标映射到其观测的泄露通道；第三，使用精确匹配的金丝雀样本验证生成文本是否真正发生泄露。案例中，作者检查了基准复现版本的DP风格方法与端到端LPRAG方法，并在电子邮件通道上测试150个金丝雀。

Result: 在作者的复现实现中，DP风格防护仅调整检索分数；生成阶段钩子为标记为TODO的桩函数，输出未变化。因此，这类方法会改变成员推断相关表现，但其生成文本命名实体泄露指标NEL_strict与无防护基线基本一致。相比之下，端到端LPRAG在电子邮件通道上通过金丝雀验证：无防护时恢复53/150个金丝雀，LPRAG时恢复0/150个。

Conclusion: 论文强调，隐私评估不应只比较单一黑盒分数，而应确认防护机制在系统中的实际作用路径，并以与目标泄露通道匹配的端到端验证补充评估。结论仅适用于作者在其技术栈上的复现，不应推广为对已发布方法或整个防护类别的普遍排序。

Abstract: Black-box privacy scores for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) are difficult to interpret unless the audited defense's active pipeline hook is known. We propose an active-path audit: inventory source-level hooks over retrieval, retrieved content, and generation; map each metric to the leakage channel it observes; and validate generated-text effects with exact-match canaries. In our benchmark reimplementations, the DP-style defenses modify retrieval scores only: their generation hooks are TODO-flagged stubs that return responses unchanged. This active path explains why they affect membership-inference behavior but track No-Defense on generated-text named-entity leakage, measured by NEL_strict. By contrast, the end-to-end LPRAG path is canary-validated on the email channel, recovering 53/150 canaries under No-Defense and 0/150 under LPRAG. These findings concern our reimplementations on our stack, not released defenses or defense families; the contribution is a methodology and case study, not a universal ranking

</details>


### [28] [Diversity Matters: Distributional Feature Coverage Sample Selection for Data-Efficient Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09047)
*Yi Yang,Xiaoke Chen,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Yu-Tong Guo,Jia-Cheng Zhao,Haiming Jin,Xiaokang Zhou,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DFCS，一种无需训练、与触发器无关的低投毒率样本选择方法，通过覆盖预训练特征空间的不同区域来提升后门攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低投毒预算下，少量投毒样本必须有效传递触发器与目标标签的关联。现有逐样本评分方法容易选到语义相近、信息冗余的样本，且部分方法依赖任务特定的替代模型训练。

Method: DFCS使用固定的预训练特征，将候选样本聚类为与投毒名额数量相同的区域，并从每个区域选取最接近聚类中心的样本。论文还通过局部一阶分析，将该策略与特征覆盖度及代表性质量两类因素联系起来。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Tiny-ImageNet和Imagenette上的BadNets与Blended攻击实验中，DFCS在全部6个数据集—攻击组合中均取得7种选择器里的最高平均攻击成功率，整体平均为96.30%；相对每个设置中最强的对比方法，平均提升4.60个百分点，同时保持干净样本准确率。

Conclusion: 特征分布覆盖而非独立样本评分，是低预算脏标签后门投毒样本选择的有效原则。DFCS以简单、训练免费且触发器无关的方式减少样本冗余，并显著提高攻击成功率。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks compromise training data so that a model retains clean accuracy but predicts an attacker-chosen target on triggered inputs. At very low poisoning rates, only a few samples convey the trigger--target association, making poison-sample selection critical. Existing methods typically rank candidates using per-sample scores, which can select redundant samples from similar semantic regions, and many require task-specific surrogate training. We propose Distributional Feature Coverage Sample Selection (DFCS), a training-free, trigger-agnostic method that clusters fixed pretrained features into one region per poisoning slot and selects the centroid-nearest sample from each region. A local first-order analysis relates this allocation to feature-coverage and representative-mass terms. Across BadNets and Blended attacks on CIFAR-10, Tiny-ImageNet, and Imagenette, DFCS achieves the highest mean attack success rate among seven selectors in all six dataset--attack settings, averaging $96.30\%$ and exceeding the strongest comparator in each setting by 4.60 percentage points on average while preserving clean accuracy. These results support distributional feature coverage as an effective selection principle for low-budget dirty-label backdoor attacks.

</details>


### [29] [SSHafe: A Real-Time SSH Brute Force Attack Detection and Novel Credential Rotation Standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09066)
*Aditya Mitra,Amar Kumar Mandal,Amaan Rais Shah,Iqra Naz,E. Fatih Yetkin,Tuğçe Ballı*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SSHafe：一个面向SSH密码认证场景的实时暴力破解检测与缓解系统，结合基于认证日志的多尺度时序特征、轻量级LightGBM分类器，以及通行密钥驱动的密码轮换流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规则系统如Fail2Ban依赖固定阈值，难以识别低频、分布式或规避阈值的尝试；常见账户恢复方式依赖邮件、一次性验证码或站外验证，存在认证绑定弱、钓鱼、会话劫持及重放等风险。因此需要同时提升SSH攻击检测能力与密码恢复流程安全性的方案。

Method: 系统从SSH认证日志中以多尺度滑动窗口提取尝试频率、到达间隔、失败比例和用户名多样性等行为特征，并使用LightGBM进行实时分类。检测到异常后，系统自动阻断目标账户，并通过SSH横幅引导合法用户进入密码轮换流程。该流程将身份认证与密码更新进行密码学绑定，不依赖会话、Cookie、邮件或一次性验证码。

Result: 在基准数据上，模型报告99.96%的检测准确率；在未标注的真实流量中也表现良好。Azure虚拟机及实时对抗流量实验显示，系统可在十秒内识别并抑制暴力破解活动，并在弱密码条件下减少账户被攻破的风险。

Conclusion: SSHafe将日志行为建模、自动阻断与无会话的密码更新机制整合，可弥补固定规则检测和传统账户恢复方案的不足。其主要贡献在于兼顾快速检测与恢复链路的认证绑定安全；但摘要未说明数据集规模、类别分布、误报率、跨环境泛化性及密码轮换协议的形式化安全验证，仍需进一步实验和审计支持。

Abstract: SSH remains a critical yet heavily targeted protocol for remote system administration, with password-based authentication exposing servers to large-scale brute-force, dictionary, and credential-spray attacks. Existing rule-based defences such as Fail2Ban fail to detect slow, distributed, or threshold-aware adversaries, while conventional account-recovery mechanisms: email links, OTPs, and out-of-band verification introduce additional vulnerabilities including phishing, session hijacking, and weak authentication binding. This work presents SSHafe, a real-time SSH brute-force detection and mitigation system that combines time-series feature engineering with a lightweight LightGBM classifier to identify attack patterns directly from system authentication logs. A multi-scale sliding-window approach extracts behavioural features such as attempt rates, inter-arrival times, failure ratios, and username diversity, enabling the model to achieve a detection accuracy of 99.96% on benchmark data and strong performance on unlabeled real-world traffic. Upon detecting an attack, SSHafe automatically blocks the targeted user account and delivers an SSH banner guiding legitimate users to a novel passkey-based password-rotation workflow. The proposed novel password reset standard performs authentication and password update in a single cryptographically bound flow, eliminating the need for sessions, cookies, OTPs, or email-based verification, and mitigating phishing, session hijacking, CSRF, and replay attacks. Experiments on an Azure VM and live adversarial traffic demonstrate that SSHafe can identify and suppress brute-force activity within ten seconds, preventing account compromise even with weak credentials.

</details>


### [30] [Telemetry and Concealment in Self-Adapting Generative AI: Logging Architecture, Adversarial Model Hiding, and the Limits of Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09069)
*Sriram Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究持续自适应生成式人工智能在部署期间自主更新参数所带来的模型风险管理问题，提出持续遥测与定期侵入式审计相结合的治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型风险管理依赖静态生命周期假设，即模型开发、独立验证和部署后不再自主变化。持续学习模型会破坏这一前提，使一次性的验证无法反映模型当前状态；同时，模型提供方可能隐瞒会触发重新验证的更新，因此需要兼顾技术可观测性与对抗性治理。

Method: 第一部分构建离散时间和连续时间并行的遥测架构：定义满足审计需求的最小充分统计量；针对离散权重更新序列构造防篡改的Merkle链；利用伊藤公式推导连续时间下的对应表示；以KL散度停止时刻设计事件驱动日志机制，以降低记录成本并保留验证意义。第二部分将提供方视为可能策略性隐瞒更新的主体，形式化提出“模型隐藏问题”，归纳六类覆盖离散与连续时间场景的规避策略，并为每类策略给出相应防护措施。

Result: 论文形成了一个双重治理机制：连续遥测可提高模型更新过程的可追溯性、篡改可检测性和异常变化识别能力，但不能单独保证治理有效；针对潜在隐瞒行为的攻击分类与对策进一步界定了遥测机制的边界。该框架不依赖具体模型架构，并试图对接传统模型风险管理中的开发、验证和治理要求。

Conclusion: 对于生产环境中持续自适应的生成式模型，模型验证必须从单次、静态的检查转变为持续观测与周期性深度审计结合的过程。遥测是必要基础，但无法替代独立、侵入式的定期审计；只有同时处理技术记录完整性和提供方的策略性规避激励，才能建立更可靠的模型风险治理体系。

Abstract: Model risk management (MRM) guidance assumes a static model lifecycle, in which models are developed, independently validated, and implemented without further autonomous modification. Continually self-adapting generative AI systems --- models that update their own weights during production deployment --- fundamentally violate this assumption and render point-in-time validation inadequate. This paper addresses the resulting governance problem in two parts. Part I develops a rigorous telemetry architecture for such models, operating simultaneously in discrete and continuous time. We establish a Minimal Sufficient Statistic for audit purposes, construct a tamper-evident Merkle chain for discrete weight sequences, derive the appropriate continuous-time generalization via the Ito formula, and propose event-driven logging via KL divergence stopping times that is both computationally tractable and meaningful for validation. Part II asks what happens when the model provider is adversarial. A firm deploying such a model has strong incentives to conceal learning updates that would trigger mandatory validation review. We formalize this as the Model Hiding Problem and provide a systematic taxonomy of six distinct attack strategies against the Part I architecture, spanning discrete and continuous time, with a formal countermeasure for each. Together the two parts establish a dual-regime architecture in which continuous telemetry is necessary but not sufficient, narrowing---but never replacing---periodic invasive audit. The framework is model-architecture-agnostic and is designed to satisfy the three pillars of traditional MRM.

</details>


### [31] [SLAC: Access-Driven CPU-to-GPU Side-channel Attacks via System-Level Cache on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09075)
*Tianhong Xu,Saion K. Roy,Ruyi Ding,Aidong Adam Ding,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了 Apple Silicon 异构 SoC 中 CPU 与 GPU 共享系统级缓存带来的细粒度跨域缓存侧信道风险，并提出 CPU 监测 GPU 工作负载缓存行为的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成平台上的 CPU-GPU 侧信道通常依赖粗粒度缓存占用竞争，或要求攻击方与目标同时驻留于 GPU。作者旨在证明：即使非特权进程仅运行于 CPU，也可利用共享 SLC 中 GPU 访问留下的缓存集合级痕迹，推断 GPU 端工作负载信息。

Method: 作者首先逆向分析 Apple M1 的 SLC 集合索引函数，以及本地私有缓存与 SLC 的交互机制。随后构建 CPrime+CProbe 技术：CPU 侧对指定 SLC 缓存集合进行填充和探测，以集合粒度监测 GPU 访问。进一步提出 GPrime+CProbe，由 GPU 协助完成更快的缓存填充，以提升隐蔽信道性能。最后将该侧信道应用于图神经网络图边重建及大语言模型输入与输出恢复实验。

Result: GPrime+CProbe 相比 CPU 侧填充方案将隐蔽信道吞吐量提升 6.4 倍。图神经网络实验中，图边重建在五个数据集上达到 90% 的准确率。语言模型实验中，对 TinyLlama 和 GPT-2 Medium，输入关键词恢复准确率最高为 94.8%，模型响应恢复准确率最高为 88.9%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，Apple Silicon 共享 SLC 会暴露此前未被充分认识的 CPU-to-GPU 细粒度缓存侧信道。异构 SoC 的缓存隔离、访问控制与缓存索引设计需要纳入跨计算域威胁模型，以降低 GPU 工作负载及其输入输出信息泄露风险。

Abstract: Modern heterogeneous System-on-Chip designs integrate CPU cores and a GPU that share a last-level cache (LLC) or system-level cache (SLC). This sharing exposes a new cross-domain attack surface, and existing attacks on integrated platforms either exploit coarse-grained cache-occupancy contention or require the adversary to co-reside on the GPU with the victim to obtain accurate timing measurements. In this work, we target Apple Silicon heterogeneous SoCs and discover that GPU memory accesses leave set-level footprints in the shared SLC, observable to an unprivileged CPU process. This keen observation enables the first fine-grained, access-driven, Prime+Probe-style CPU-to-GPU cache side-channel attacks against GPU workloads. We first reverse-engineer the Apple M1 SLC set-indexing functions and the interactions between local private caches and the SLC. Building on these findings, we construct the CPrime+CProbe SLC side-channel technique, which monitors GPU victim activity from the CPU at cache-set granularity. We then introduce an accelerated variant, GPrime+CProbe, in which an adversary leverages the GPU for faster SLC priming, yielding a 6.4x increase in the covert-channel throughput. Lastly, we demonstrate two end-to-end privacy attacks using the new side-channels: a graph-edge reconstruction attack on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) that achieves 90% edge accuracy across five datasets, and an LLM privacy attack that recovers input keywords with up to 94.8% accuracy and model responses with up to 88.9% accuracy across TinyLlama and GPT-2 Medium models. Our results reveal a new class of microarchitectural vulnerabilities in Apple Silicon and call for secure system cache designs for heterogeneous SoCs.

</details>


### [32] [You Are Not My Teammate: Behavioral Fingerprint-based Detection of Suspicious Account Misuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09132)
*Dong Hwan Lee,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向MOBA游戏账号共享与代练行为的检测方法，通过比较同一账号历史与近期游戏行为的变化，构建行为指纹以识别异常账号使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏安全研究多聚焦于外挂机器人和游戏币交易，但在以胜负和排名为核心的MOBA游戏中，账号共享与代练会直接破坏竞技公平性。由于此类行为的标注样本稀缺，需要一种不依赖大量标签的鲁棒检测方案。

Method: 方法以玩家游戏行为为基础建立行为指纹，并量化账号历史行为与近期行为之间的差异。通过比较账号内行为指纹的一致性及账号间指纹的可区分性，识别可能由不同操作者导致的异常行为变化，从而检测账号共享和代练。

Result: 实验表明，同一账号内部的行为指纹与不同账号之间的行为指纹具有可区分性。该结果支持在标注数据有限的条件下，对可疑账号共享和代练行为进行快速识别。

Conclusion: 行为指纹能够有效刻画玩家相对稳定的操作与对局行为特征；当账号操作者发生变化时，行为分布的显著偏移可作为风险信号。该方法适合标签稀缺的游戏安全场景，但摘要未说明具体特征、阈值设定、误报率及跨版本泛化能力，仍需完整实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Online games have been continuously affected by cyber threats such as game bots and gold farming. Game bots, which are automated programs that play on behalf of human users, significantly accelerate character progression and reduce the engagement of legitimate players, potentially leading to user churn. In addition, gold farming enables the monetization of in-game currency into real-world money, resulting in unfair profits. For these reasons, prior studies have primarily focused on detecting game bots and gold farming. However, in competitive Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) games such as League of Legends, match outcomes and rankings are the primary objectives, where individual performance is more critical than in-game economic factors. Accordingly, account misuse such as account sharing and boosting has emerged as a major threat to fair competition. In this study, we propose a behavioral fingerprint-based detection method. Our approach analyzes and quantifies changes between a player's historical and recent in-game behaviors. Consequently, it enables the robust identification of suspicious account sharing and boosting, even in label-scarce environments. Experimental results show that behavioral fingerprints within the same account are distinguishable from those across different accounts, supporting rapid detection of suspicious account misuse even with limited labeled data.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Direct Identifiers: Probabilistic Privacy Risk Estimation for Privacy-Conscious LLM Query Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09140)
*Li Siyan,Zhou Yu,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出在隐私感知委托（PCD）框架中引入由大语言模型估计的概率化k-匿名性指标，以缓解仅检测显式个人可识别信息（PII）而忽略无PII自我披露所带来的重识别风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户—LLM隐私保护方法主要处理姓名、地址等可被常规检测器识别的直接标识符，但用户透露的职业、经历、习惯等准标识符也可能经组合后暴露身份。PCD虽通过本地模型充当中介进行隐私处理，但原有目标未充分刻画这类间接重识别风险，因此需要补充可操作的隐私度量。

Method: 作者构建PUPA-SD数据集，收录包含自然化自我披露的用户查询；在概率化PCD设置下，将LLM驱动的k-匿名性概率估计纳入优化目标，用于评估文本中的准标识符组合是否可能将用户缩小到较小候选群体。该方法称为PAPILLON，并在多种本地模型上考察隐私保护与回答质量之间的权衡及跨对话泛化表现。

Result: 初步实验表明，在PUPA-SD上优化PAPILLON后，模型在未见对话上的输出质量得到改善。不同本地模型的表现存在明显差异：Llama-3.2-3B取得最佳隐私—效用平衡；参数更小的模型则难以同时维持回答质量和隐私保护目标。

Conclusion: k-匿名性可作为PCD的重要辅助指标，用于补足显式PII检测无法覆盖的自我披露与准标识符风险。论文的核心贡献在于提供相应数据集和概率估计式优化思路；不过摘要所述结果仍属初步结论，后续应进一步验证LLM作为匿名性估计器的可靠性、不同人群与领域下的泛化能力，以及该指标与真实重识别风险之间的一致性。

Abstract: Recent work on protecting privacy during user-LLM interactions often focuses on direct, explicit identifiers: the personally-identifiable information (PII) captured by standard detectors.
