One such approach is Privacy-Conscious Delegation (PCD), where a local LLM acts as an intermediary. However, privacy risk does not stem solely from explicit identifiers but also PII-free self-disclosures, leaving users identifiable through combinations of quasi-identifying traits. We investigate a probabilistic variant of PCD, where we augment its objectives with an LLM-driven probabilistic estimation of k-anonymity. To facilitate this, we first create the PUPA-SD dataset, which contains naturalistic user queries with self-disclosure. Our preliminary results indicate that optimizing PAPILLON on PUPA-SD improves quality on unseen conversations across a variety of local models and produces the best privacy-utility balance for Llama-3.2-3B, while smaller models struggle to jointly optimize quality and privacy. We propose k-anonymity as a useful auxiliary metric for tackling PCD.

</details>


### [34] [Governing the KV Cache: Preventing Timing Side-Channel Leakage in Multi-Tenant LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09225)
*Tejasvi C. Addagada*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出KVGov治理层，以按主体派生的HMAC盐值隔离多租户LLM的KV缓存键，旨在消除跨主体前缀缓存命中延迟泄露；同时通过审计调度与稳定性分析补充治理机制，并声称在边界注入盐值可兼顾隔离与缓存复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享KV缓存可显著降低首个令牌生成时间，但不同主体间的缓存命中与未命中具有可观测延迟差异，可能泄露私有提示词的前缀信息。现有系统若直接跨主体复用缓存，缺少以身份边界为单位的密码学隔离机制。

Method: KVGov为每个主体计算σ_p=HMAC_K(secret, principal_id)，并将其纳入块哈希链，使不同主体的缓存键在密码学上分离。论文通过1000次固定种子消融实验主张该盐值是防护所必需且充分的组成；进一步提出ORIGAMI审计调度器，以Stackelberg水位分配降低潜在攻击收益，并给出全局缓存策略的演化稳定性阈值分析。实验部分在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、vLLM和A100上测量缓存延迟差异，并在llama.cpp与Apple Metal环境复现；防御效果主要使用由实测数据校准的模拟评估。

Result: 实机测得冷缓存与命中缓存的TTFT比值为0.22，独立软件栈上为0.093，说明延迟信号明显。论文报告ORIGAMI可降低12.6%的预期对手收益，并提出31.6%的主体比例阈值；在仅于提示词分歧边界注入盐值的设计下，估计仍可保留93%的前缀缓存收益。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将主体身份绑定到KV缓存键，实现对跨主体缓存侧信道的直接隔离。论文的主要待验证点在于：盐值注入整个哈希链通常会阻断跨主体共享，而“仅在分歧边界注入”如何同时保证不存在可观测共享信号、并保留93%收益，需要给出精确的缓存键构造、威胁模型与端到端实测；此外，防御结论以模拟为主，宜补充真实多主体并发环境下的攻击成功率、吞吐、TTFT尾延迟和内存开销评测。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the primary throughput optimization in modern large language model (LLM) inference, enabling prefix reuse across requests. In multi-tenant deployments this cache is shared across tenants, creating a timing side channel: an adversarial tenant can reconstruct another tenant's private prompt by probing cache-hit latency. Three published attacks exploit it -- PROMPTPEEK, EarlyBird and InputSnatch -- reaching up to 100% attack success rate against unprotected vLLM and SGLang, with rates varying by cache architecture and prompt structure.
  We present KVGov, a governance layer addressing all three attack families' prefix-cache paths under one mechanism. A per-principal salt sigma_p = HMAC_K(secret, principal_id) seeds the block-hash chain, making cache keys cryptographically disjoint across principals. An ablation (N=1000 trials, seed 2026, deterministic judges) isolates this salt as the necessary and sufficient component. KVGov adds ORIGAMI, a Stackelberg water-filling audit scheduler that reduces adversary expected utility by 12.6% at realistic tenant heterogeneity (Gini 0.63), and an evolutionary stability analysis giving a 31.6% adversary-prevalence tipping point below which global caching remains stable.
  On real hardware (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, vLLM 0.26.0, NVIDIA A100) we measure a gate-verified cold/cached TTFT ratio of 0.22, confirming the channel is exploitable at production scale; the defense itself is evaluated in simulation calibrated to those measurements. We replicate the channel on an independent stack (llama.cpp on Apple Metal, ratio 0.093). Finally, isolation and cache efficiency need not conflict: identifying information resides only where prompts diverge, so injecting the salt at that boundary rather than the chain root retains an estimated 93% of the prefix-cache benefit with no cross-principal signal.

</details>


### [35] [Track me if you can: Ephemeral coin tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09249)
*Ignacio Amores-Sesar,Christian Cachin,Rohit Chatterjee,Luiza Soezima,François-Xavier Wicht,Michelle Yeo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出短暂性货币追踪（ECT）原语，通过可衰减的追踪标签，对支付系统中的追踪范围实施密码学上的硬性限制，避免监管机构无限扩展监控或永久追踪用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私支付系统的追踪方案通常赋予监管机构不受限制的能力：追踪可沿交易图无限传播，或持续覆盖用户未来交易，最终可能导致整个账本去匿名化。此类能力过度依赖监管机构自律，与受监管支付基础设施对隐私边界和可问责性的需求不符。

Method: 形式化定义ECT及其安全性、隐私性保证。系统由监管机构签发追踪标签，标签在每次交易跳转后逐步衰减；达到协议规定的跳数后，标签坍缩为与未标记货币不可区分的值。ECT同时限制可并行追踪的用户数量和每条追踪的最大跳数，并分别基于指数ElGamal加密和Damgård–Jurik加密给出两种构造。

Result: 所提出的机制在协议层面保证追踪预算不可被监管机构突破：即使监管机构具有恶意动机，也无法让标签超过预设跳数继续传播。两种基于不同公钥加密体系的构造展示了ECT的可实现性，并提供相应的安全与隐私保证。

Conclusion: ECT将有限、临时且可验证的追踪能力嵌入隐私支付协议，为CBDC、机构稳定币等合规支付系统提供了在反金融犯罪需求与用户隐私之间进行技术平衡的方法。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving payment systems are well understood, yet their adoption in regulated settings, such as central bank digital currencies (CBDCs), institutional stablecoins, and other compliant payment infrastructures, has been limited by concerns over their potential misuse for illicit activities. Regulators counter financial crime with a toolbox of complementary measures to identify, trace, and stop criminal actors. Tracing is one key tool: acting on outside evidence that a user is implicated in a crime such as money laundering, law enforcement follows the suspect's funds through the ledger to uncover laundering routes and accomplices. The tracing schemes proposed in the literature, however, grant authorities unbounded capabilities: once initiated, tracing propagates through the transaction graph or persists across all future transactions of a user, and may eventually deanonymize the entire ledger. Only the goodwill of the authority, or the honesty of a committee, keeps surveillance targeted and temporary.
  We introduce ephemeral coin tracing (ECT), a primitive whose tracing capacity is bounded by construction, both in the number of simultaneously traced users and in the number of hops each trace survives. The authority issues tracing tags that degrade at each hop; after a protocol-defined number of hops, a tag collapses into a value indistinguishable from that of an untagged coin. Within a tracing period the bound is absolute: no authority, however motivated, can follow a tag past its budget. We formalize ECT, define its security and privacy guarantees, and give two constructions, one over exponential ElGamal and one over Damgård--Jurik encryption.

</details>


### [36] [DiffSafeMerge: Mitigating Backdoor Inheritance in Diffusion Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09445)
*Jiayang Zhang,Ji Guo,Jiachen Li,Wenshu Fan,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DiffSafeMerge（DSM），用于在未知受损来源、触发器和目标的条件下，降低无条件扩散模型检查点合并时的休眠后门迁移风险，同时尽量保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检查点合并通常假设所有来源模型可信，但公开模型检查点可能包含隐蔽后门：模型在正常生成时表现良好，却会在特定条件下输出攻击者指定结果。若进行全面清洗，虽可能缓解风险，却容易显著损害图像质量；而实际场景通常无法预先识别受损模型及其后门模式。

Method: DSM使用一个小规模、无标签的干净数据集以及固定的攻击无关压力探针，对各来源检查点的模型块进行风险评分。对于可疑块，方法将其合并贡献向可信参考模型收缩，并在预设的干净数据去噪损失预算内选择衰减强度，以兼顾安全性与生成保真度。

Result: 实验覆盖4种攻击、2个数据集和21种目标条件。在14个来源案例中，原本预期的合并方案已有10个实现最差目标攻击成功率（ASR）为0，DSM保持了这些结果；其余4个案例中，DSM在3个随机种子下均未出现目标匹配，其中3例的基线ASR为48%至100%。在两个数据集上均达到最差目标ASR为0的方法中，DSM在匹配的seed-0比较下获得最低的案例平均FID。

Conclusion: DSM表明，基于无标签干净样本、攻击无关探针和分块自适应衰减的检查点合并策略，能够在不依赖已知后门信息的情况下有效抑制后门迁移，并在安全性与图像生成质量之间取得较优平衡。

Abstract: Unconditional diffusion checkpoint merging assumes benign sources, yet a compromised public checkpoint can transfer a dormant backdoor while clean generation appears normal. Mitigation is difficult without knowing the compromised source, trigger, or target, and broad sanitization may degrade image quality. We introduce DiffSafeMerge (DSM), which uses a small unlabeled clean set and fixed, attack-agnostic stress probes to score source blocks, shrink suspicious contributions toward a trusted reference, and select attenuation under a clean denoising-loss budget. We evaluate four attacks, two datasets, and 21 target conditions. Intended merging already has zero worst-target ASR in 10 of 14 source cases; DSM preserves these outcomes and records no target match in the remaining four over three seeds, including three with baseline ASR of 48--100\%. Among methods with zero worst-target ASR on both datasets, DSM obtains the lowest case-averaged FID in the matched seed-0 comparison.

</details>


### [37] [ActBench: Self-Evolving Benchmark of Behavioral Safety in Cowork Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09476)
*Hongwei Yao,Yiming Liu,Meihui Chen,Jieling Chen,Zikun Chen,Yiling He,Wangze Ni,Cong Wang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ActBench，用于评估协作型智能体在执行轨迹中泄露受保护数据、修改未授权状态或调用未授权API等行为安全风险，并通过奖励引导束搜索与双重证据验证系统化测试攻击效果和任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评估主要关注最终响应，难以发现执行过程中发生的隐蔽违规行为。为弥补这一缺陷，研究需要在保持原始任务、配置、初始状态、评分模型和可信记录不变的条件下，评估可由任务触发的行为风险，并同时衡量任务完成效用。

Method: 构建包含600个测试案例、213个场景、15类风险行为、6类执行空间和48个Web服务API的ActBench。每个案例将良性任务与注入可达载荷的对抗变体配对。提出奖励引导的束搜索方法，联合优化攻击有效性与任务效用，并利用反思机制定位失败的执行检查点、迭代修改载荷。进一步采用双重证据验证机制，结合执行日志证据与基于大语言模型的轨迹证据评估安全性和任务效用。

Result: 在24,000条执行轨迹上评估了15个大语言模型和6个开源协作型智能体。在固定执行框架下，不同模型的攻击成功率为10.1%至94.4%；在固定基础模型下，不同智能体的攻击成功率为73.7%至94.4%。结果表明，模型之间的差异大于智能体执行框架之间的差异，但所有测试框架中的攻击成功率整体都较高。

Conclusion: ActBench能够从执行轨迹而非最终响应出发，系统揭示协作型智能体的行为安全缺陷。实验显示，相关风险具有较强普遍性，基础模型能力可能是主要影响因素，而仅依赖执行框架难以充分降低风险。该基准可用于智能体安全评测、攻击生成和防御研究。

Abstract: Cowork agents may complete benign tasks while disclosing protected data, manipulating unauthorized state, invocate unauthorized API. We define behavioral safety and introduce ActBench, a self-evolving benchmark that evaluates such behavior risk from execution trajectories rather than final responses. Each case pairs a benign task with an adversarial variant that preserves its instruction, configuration, initial state, rating model, and trusted records while injecting a task-reachable payload. ActBench contains 600 cases from 213 scenarios, spanning 15 risk behaviors, six execution spaces, and 48 web-service APIs.To move beyond static payloads, we propose a reward-guided beam search method that jointly optimizes attack effectiveness and task utility, while reflection diagnoses failed execution checkpoint and guides payload revision. Besides, we propose a dual evidence verification mechanism that verifies agent execution safety and utility through log evidence and LLM-based trajectory evidence.We evaluate 15 LLMs and 6 open-source cowork agents over 24,000 trajectories. Under a fixed harness, attack success rates ranges from 10.1% to 94.4% across models, while under a fixed base model, they range from 73.7% to 94.4% across agents.These results show greater variation across models than agent harness, while attacks remain highly successful across all tested harnesses.Our benchmark is released at: https://github.com/zjuicsr/ActBench.

</details>


### [38] [ANTMAN: An Efficient and Interpretable RTL-Level Run-Time Detection Framework for Stealthy Branch Predictor Attacks on BOOM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09498)
*Muhammad Hassan,Maria Mushtaq,Jaan Raik,Tara Ghasempouri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向BOOM RISC-V处理器的硬件级运行时检测方案，用于识别隐蔽的分支预测器侧信道攻击。该方案基于离线提取的关联规则，并将规则嵌入非侵入式硬件监视器，实现快速、可解释且零误报的在线检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RISC-V微架构侧信道攻击检测研究主要集中于离线分析，运行时检测仍不充分。基于硬件性能计数器的方法受限于计数器数量，并在检测精度、速度和采样粒度之间存在权衡；离散采样还会遗漏不同微架构模块之间的中间关联关系。此外，x86和ARM的专有指令集限制了研究者对处理器微架构的修改。

Method: 作者在BOOM RISC-V处理器上设计了安全可控、可解释且非侵入式的RTL级运行时检测方案，并在简化的Next-Line Predictor（NLP）和复杂的TAGE预测器配置下进行评估。方法首先离线提取分支预测器攻击相关的关联规则，再将规则硬件化为规则监视器，以便在运行时持续检测异常行为并及时终止执行。

Result: 该方案能够快速检测攻击，在秘密信息泄露前终止执行，并实现零误报。同时，即使面对同一攻击家族中此前未见过的变体，也保持了较好的检测灵活性。

Conclusion: 将离线关联规则分析与RTL级非侵入式硬件监视器结合，可有效弥补RISC-V平台运行时微架构攻击检测的不足。该方案兼顾检测速度、可解释性和泛化能力，为隐蔽型分支预测器侧信道攻击提供了实用的安全防护路径。

Abstract: Runtime detection of microarchitectural side channel attacks remains significantly underexplored in RISCV compared with x86 and ARM ISAs, posing a serious threat to critical applications. State-of-the-art branch predictor attacks bypass traditional data and instruction caches by directly exploiting the state of internal history tables, making them inherently stealthy. Recent research has explored offline detection of microarchitectural attacks on RISC-V; however, efficient runtime detection of microarchitectural attacks on RISC-V hardware remains significantly unaddressed. State-of-the-art hardware-based runtime detection solutions leverage hardware performance counters (HPCs) but suffer from a restricted set of counter registers and tradeoffs between detection accuracy, detection speed, and sampling granularity, making them impractical for stealthy attacks. Moreover, sampling HPCs after distinct intervals leaves intermediate relationships between different microarchitectural blocks unobserved. Additionally, proprietary x86 and ARM ISAs constrain researchers from modifying processor microarchitectural designs. To address these limitations, we propose the first secure-by-design, highly interpretable, non-intrusive, RTL-level runtime detection solution for stealthy branch predictor attacks on BOOM RISC-V, evaluated under both simplified Next-Line Predictor (NLP) and complex TAGE predictor configurations. The attack detection relies on association rules extracted offline and embedded in hardware as a non-intrusive rule monitor that enables runtime detection. The proposed approach achieves excellent detection speed, terminates execution before secret disclosure, and produces zero false positives while remaining flexible for detecting previously unseen variants within the same family of branch predictor attacks.

</details>


### [39] [STAIR: Effective Incident Response Using an End-to-End Agentic Planning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09524)
*Hanlin Jiang,Jionghao Huang,Shaofei Li,Bojia Yu,Peng Jiang,Yuxin Ren,Ning Jia,Yao Guo,Ding Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIR，一种面向软件系统事件响应的端到端智能体规划框架，通过图状态表示、阶段化路由、历史经验检索和执行反馈闭环，提升复杂事件恢复的适应性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统专家驱动的固定响应剧本难以适应事件状态变化、恢复目标调整和执行反馈。现有基于大语言模型的规划器与工具使用智能体在长时程响应中仍缺乏统一的状态维护、阶段对齐机制和历史经验复用能力。

Method: STAIR将当前事件建模为Graph-as-State，以持续维护事件状态；通过Stage Router将任务分派给面向不同恢复阶段的专用智能体；利用历史经验检索辅助动作选择；由Execution Harness执行动作、返回反馈、更新事件状态，并验证动作效果以支持后续经验复用。

Result: 在100个基于Docker的网络靶场中，STAIR取得0.94的归一化防御得分，相较最强基线提升9.5%。该结果表明，框架在事件响应规划和恢复效果方面具有较强性能。

Conclusion: STAIR通过统一状态表示、阶段专门化规划、经验检索和执行反馈闭环，缓解了长时程事件响应中的适应性与稳定性问题，为自动化软件系统恢复提供了一种有效的智能体规划方案。

Abstract: Incident response planning is critical for restoring compromised software systems after cyberattacks. Common practice relies on expert-driven playbooks that encode fixed response procedures, but these static workflows struggle to adapt to evolving incident states, changing recovery objectives, and execution feedback. Recent LLM-based planners and tool-using agents improve automation, yet they remain unstable in long-horizon response because they lack a unified basis for maintaining incident state, aligning actions with the current recovery stage, and reusing historical experience.
  We present STAIR, an end-to-end agentic planning framework for incident response. The framework maintains the current incident as Graph-as-State, uses a Stage Router to dispatch planning to stage-specialized agents, and retrieves historical experiences to guide action selection. An Execution Harness executes actions, returns feedback to update the incident state, and validates action effects for future experience reuse. Across 100 Docker-based cyber ranges, our framework achieves a normalized defense score of 0.94 and improves over the strongest baseline by 9.5%.

</details>


### [40] [RangeFactory: Scalable Construction of Multi-Hop Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09526)
*Hanlin Jiang,Puyi Wang,Jiandong Jin,Shaofei Li,Zhan Shen,Pengli Wang,Ziming Wang,Yifeng Cai,Ning Jia,Yuxin Ren,Peng Jiang,Yao Guo,Ding Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出RangeFactory，用于将孤立漏洞环境自动编排为经过端到端验证的多跳网络靶场，并据此构建大规模基准RangeBench以评估智能体的持续任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已扩展单点漏洞任务或通过人工语义构造多主机场景，但难以自动将不断增加的漏洞环境组合为可验证的完整多跳任务链。论文旨在填补从初始入口成功到持续完成后续路径之间的评估与构建缺口。

Method: RangeFactory将靶场构建建模为依赖解析问题：首先从智能体针对真实漏洞的实际执行中提取依赖信息；其次利用模板引导的编排机制处理已知依赖；最后通过端到端执行验证组合后显现的运行时依赖。基于该框架，作者构建RangeBench，并收集带结果标注的多跳执行轨迹。

Result: RangeBench包含1,148个经验证的靶场实例、287条不同任务链。实验从任务深度、网络规模和任务信息量等维度评估前沿智能体。在已完成入口漏洞利用的运行中，仍有24.5%至47.0%未能完成后续路径。框架还生成5,541条带结果标注的多跳轨迹。

Conclusion: 论文表明，自动化依赖解析与端到端验证能够有效扩展多跳靶场构建规模；同时，智能体在取得初始进展后仍普遍缺乏持续完成长程任务链的能力。RangeBench及其轨迹数据可作为后续过程分析、能力评测与训练研究的基础资源。

Abstract: Real-world cyberattacks often require sustained progress across multiple hosts and network segments, making multi-hop cyber ranges essential infrastructure for studying and improving LLM agents' ability to sustain complete attack chains. Prior work has scaled isolated vulnerability tasks and constructed multi-host scenarios from manually specified vulnerability semantics. However, they are still unable to automatically orchestrate the growing supply of vulnerability environments into end-to-end validated multi-hop ranges. To this end, we present RangeFactory, an automated cyber-range orchestration framework that constructs multi-hop cyber ranges at scale from isolated vulnerability environments. RangeFactory formulates range construction as dependency resolution: it extracts dependency information from agents' actual attacks against real vulnerabilities, resolves known dependencies through template-guided orchestration, and uses end-to-end attack execution to validate runtime dependencies that emerge after composition. Using RangeFactory, we construct RangeBench with 1,148 validated range instances spanning 287 distinct attack chains and evaluate frontier attack agents across attack depth, network scale, and task information. Among runs that compromise the entry vulnerability, 24.5-47.0% still fail to complete the remaining attack path, revealing a substantial sustained-compromise gap between establishing an initial foothold and completing a multi-hop attack. RangeFactory further produces a corpus of 5,541 outcome-annotated multi-hop attack trajectories, providing execution data for attack-process analysis and future agent training.

</details>


### [41] [From Runnable to Verifiable: An Independent Reproducibility Study of LLM/Agent-Driven Vulnerability Validation Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09567)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究对2023—2026年由大语言模型/智能体驱动的漏洞研究文献开展预注册可复现性审计，发现公开、可运行、能产生信号且语义确认的安全研究工件之间存在显著落差；许多工件无法完成声明流程，且内置判定器容易产生误报和错误归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将代码仓库或触发信号视为漏洞复现证据，但缺乏对工件可获取性、可执行性、信号有效性及语义确认程度的系统测量。研究旨在量化这些差距，并建立可复用的安全研究可复现性评估协议。

Method: 通过双人筛选构建包含104篇论文的共识语料库，其中59篇提供公开可访问工件。研究执行了18篇论文工件的R0/R1复现评估，以及锚定基准中的102个案例；对30个产生信号的案例进行修复后反事实测试，对19个案例进行匹配的良性负控测试。评估采用预注册后置条件、环境修复阶梯、G1—G3语义证据等级及修复后反事实判定器。

Result: 仅10/18（55.6%）的论文级工件在原始环境R0完成声明流程，经过仅环境层面的R1修复后升至11/18（61.1%）。锚定基准中58/102（56.9%）的案例存在脚本内部CVE标识与目录声明CVE不一致的问题。在修复后反事实测试中，20/30（66.7%）仍产生原有信号；19个匹配负控中有7个（36.8%）对良性输入触发。内置判定器灵敏度为60%，特异度为45%。

Conclusion: 仅在易受攻击版本上触发信号，不能证明实现了特定CVE的可靠复现；必须结合干净的修复后反事实测试和更高等级的语义证据。研究结果虽属探索性，但表明安全研究工件的可复现性与判定可靠性仍不足，其预注册评估协议可作为后续研究的通用模板。

Abstract: Security research artifacts---repositories, PoC exploits, and validation pipelines---are increasingly produced by LLM/agent-driven vulnerability workflows, yet the gap between \emph{publicly available}, \emph{runnable}, \emph{signal-producing}, and \emph{semantically confirmed} artifacts is poorly measured. We conduct a pre-registered reproducibility audit of this literature. A search covering 2023--2026 with dual screening yields a 104-paper consensus corpus, of which 59 papers (56.7\%) have a publicly reachable artifact. We execute an 18-paper sample at R0/R1 and all 102 cases of the anchor benchmark (arXiv:2509.24037), with patched-counterfactual verdicts on 30 signal-producing cases and matched-negative-control verdicts on 19. Three findings stand out. First, 58/102 (56.9\%) anchor cases contain a script-internal CVE identifier that diverges from the declared directory CVE. Second, only 10/18 (55.6\%) paper-level artifacts complete their declared workflow at R0, rising to 11/18 (61.1\%) after environment-only R1 repair. Third, artifact-embedded oracles prove unreliable: 20/30 patched-counterfactual audits still produce the claimed signal on the patched build, 7/19 matched negative controls still trigger on benign input, and the oracle confusion matrix has sensitivity 60\% and specificity 45\%. A trigger on the vulnerable build is not evidence of CVE-specific reproduction without a clean patched counterfactual. These are exploratory results from a pre-registered protocol, and our protocol---pre-registered post-conditions, R0/R1 repair ladder, G1--G3 semantic evidence levels, and patched-counterfactual oracles---is a reusable template for the security reproducibility community.

</details>


### [42] [Activation Probes Surface Code-Security Signals that the Model's Output Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09643)
*Ivan Wiryadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，开源代码审查模型的内部激活包含可检测代码安全漏洞的信号，而直接提示模型输出判断往往无法识别漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能编码代理生成的生产代码不断增加，人工安全审查难以扩展；同时，广泛使用的编码代理多为闭源模型，部署团队无法检查其内部机制，因此需要探索利用可读的开源审查模型激活来提升漏洞检测能力。

Method: 研究者在成对的Python漏洞函数与修复函数语料上，为五个开源审查模型分别训练单一线性探针；随后在训练阶段未覆盖漏洞类型的真实公开漏洞上进行零样本测试，并将探针结果与模型基于提示词输出的YES/NO判断、书面 verdict 及链式思考结果进行比较。

Result: 对于通过修改单个函数修复的漏洞，线性探针在所有模型上有61%至67%的概率为漏洞函数赋予高于修复函数的分数，显著超过50%的随机基线。在所有测试提示下，探针均优于从模型logits读取的YES/NO判断。模型生成的书面结论通常对漏洞函数和修复函数给出相同答案，因此难以区分二者。

Conclusion: 模型内部激活蕴含提示式问答未能提取的代码安全信号。对开源审查模型训练线性探针，可能是检测未知类型软件漏洞、辅助审查闭源编码代理输出的有效方法。

Abstract: AI coding agents now write a growing share of production code, and human security review does not scale at the rate code is generated. The agents in widest use are closed-weight, so a deploying team cannot read their internals. It can instead run an open-weight model as a reviewer over the agent's output. That reviewer's activations are readable. We ask whether reading those activations recovers a security signal that simply asking the same reviewer misses. We fit a single linear probe per model on a corpus of paired vulnerable-and-fixed Python functions, then test it without retraining on real disclosed vulnerabilities whose weakness type the probe never saw in training, across five open-weight reviewer models. On the vulnerabilities fixed by changing a single function, the probe scores the vulnerable function above its fix on 61-67% of cases for every model, beating the 50% chance line. It also beats the same model's prompted YES/NO win-rate read from its logits, under every prompt we try. Asking the model for a written verdict, even with chain-of-thought, returns the same answer on the vulnerable and fixed function most of the time and so cannot tell them apart. Model activations carry a code-security signal that prompting the same model misses.

</details>


### [43] [Full-Key Recovery and Forgery from One MQOM v2.1 Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09699)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文声称针对 MQOM v2.1 构造了一种仅利用一份已接受签名即可恢复完整签名私钥的攻击，并可进一步生成能通过参考验证器的新消息签名。作者给出不同安全类别下的复杂度估计，认为均低于相应安全基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验 MQOM v2.1 的签名流程中见证值前缀、Merkle 路径奇偶关系、隐藏叶承诺以及纠错信息之间是否存在可被单个公开签名利用的代数关联，从而评估其针对单签名密钥恢复的安全性。

Method: 方法首先由签名中的兄弟路径导出公开值 A，并利用树奇偶关系得到 s=δ⊕A；再代入隐藏叶承诺，形成 Enc_K(δ⊕A)=T⊕LinOrtho(δ) 的公开约束。攻击者枚举或扫描 δ 的候选空间，借助同一签名内的纠错信息补全完整见证，再使用公开 MQ 关系筛选能对应有效签名密钥的候选。实现层面采用 AES/Rijndael 指定电路、Gray 遍历与保留电路状态降低全域扫描成本；同时在参考实现上进行缩减域实验，并以理想密码模型的阶乘矩界评估额外伪前像。

Result: 作者报告：类别 I 与 V 的完整域扫描复杂度分别约为 2^142.335112 和 2^271.794162 个布尔门；类别 III 的两种扫描覆盖比例分别为 1/2+2^-20 与 0.580004770183，对应成本约为 2^206.774558 和 2^206.988685 个布尔门。论文声称这些数值均低于 NIST 安全基准。缩减域实验中，攻击在三个类别均恢复了逐字节一致的见证与密钥，并生成了可被参考验证器接受的新消息签名。

Conclusion: 若文中的推导、复杂度模型和实验可复现且无遗漏，该结果意味着 MQOM v2.1 存在由单个有效签名触发的完整私钥恢复风险，而不仅是局部伪造风险。其核心问题在于公开签名记录固定了足以建立可搜索方程的常量；即使通过与盐绑定的全局根扩展改变这些常量，也未消除该恢复通道。后续评估应重点复核方程推导、搜索覆盖率与成本口径、候选筛选的误报概率，以及参考实现与规范实现之间的一致性。

Abstract: We give a full-key-recovery attack on MQOM v2.1, a Round-3 candidate in the NIST additional-signature process, that recovers the complete signing key from one accepted signature and uses it to sign a fresh message. If $δ=\operatorname{FirstBits}_λ(x)$ is the prefix of the witness $x$, the sibling path determines a public value $A$ such that tree parity gives $s=δ\oplus A$. Substitution into the hidden-leaf commitment yields $$\mathsf{Enc}_K(δ\oplus A)=T\oplus\mathsf{LinOrtho}(δ)$$ with public values $K$ and $T$. The correction in the same signature expands a solution into a complete witness, while the public MQ relation identifies those yielding valid signing keys; serializing such a witness gives the secret key, enabling a fresh-message signature accepted by the reference verifier.
  We evaluate this equation over the specified AES/Rijndael circuits using retained circuit state along a Gray traversal. Complete-domain scans for Categories I and V cost $2^{142.335112}$ and $2^{271.794162}$ Boolean gates. Category-III scans cover $1/2+2^{-20}$ and $0.580004770183$ of the domain at costs of $2^{206.774558}$ and $2^{206.988685}$ gates. All four totals are below the NIST security benchmarks. Reduced-domain runs against the reference implementation recover the byte-exact witness and key in all three categories and produce a fresh-message forgery accepted by the reference verifier. Independently generated source-syntax circuits evaluate the fixed ciphers over the stated domains and translated L3 prefixes, while an exact ideal-cipher factorial-moment bound controls additional equation preimages passed to public-key validation. Every value in the equation is fixed by the accepted transcript, so salt-bound global-root expansion changes its public constants without removing the one-signature recovery channel.

</details>

*Puyu Zeng,Simeng Qin,Jingzhi Li,Ju Jia,Zheli Liu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体技能生态中的跨技能协同攻击问题，提出攻击框架ColluSkill和防御系统ChainGuard。实验表明，ColluSkill平均攻击成功率达96.0%，ChainGuard可将其降至22.5%，同时保持99.5%的良性工作流通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能扫描器主要独立检查单个技能，难以识别多个看似合理的技能在组合执行后形成的恶意工作流。这种跨技能组合盲点使攻击者能够将完整恶意意图拆分并隐藏在多个技能中。

Method: ColluSkill将恶意意图分解为相互依赖的子载荷，并通过上下文依赖、工件传递和执行交接构造多技能攻击链；同时利用大语言模型进行链规划，并依据扫描器反馈优化子技能以降低单点可疑信号。ChainGuard则联合分析候选技能与已安装技能，重建跨技能依赖、工件流、能力组合及下游行为，从工作流层面检测风险。

Result: 在六种代表性技能扫描器上的实验显示，ColluSkill平均攻击成功率为96.0%，持续优于单技能和多技能攻击基线。ChainGuard将攻击成功率降低至22.5%，并使99.5%的良性工作流正常通过。

Conclusion: LLM智能体技能安全不能仅依赖单技能检测，必须引入面向技能链和工作流上下文的联合分析。ColluSkill验证了跨技能协同攻击的现实威胁，ChainGuard则证明链级安全防护能够在保持较高可用性的同时显著降低攻击风险。

Abstract: Agent skills are emerging as an important attack surface in LLM-based agent systems. Through an empirical study of existing skill scanners, we find that current defenses mainly inspect individual skills, leaving risks from cross-skill composition insufficiently examined. This creates a practical blind spot: multiple locally plausible skills may pass security checks while collectively forming a harmful workflow during agent execution. To investigate this threat, we propose ColluSkill, a collusive multi-skill-chain attack framework that decomposes a complete malicious intent into interdependent sub-payloads embedded in independently packaged skills. The attack does not rely on any single malicious skill, but emerges from the ordered composition of locally plausible behaviors through contextual dependencies, artifact passing, and execution handoffs. ColluSkill further employs LLM-based chain planning and scanner-feedback refinement to preserve chain-level attack semantics while reducing suspicious signals in individual sub-skills. To defend against such attacks, we propose ChainGuard, a context-aware skill-chain scanner that jointly analyzes a candidate skill and the skills already installed in the agent environment. ChainGuard reconstructs cross-skill dependencies, artifact flows, capability compositions, and downstream behaviors to identify risks that emerge only at the workflow level. Experiments on six representative skill scanners show that ColluSkill achieves an average attack success rate of 96.0% and consistently outperforms the evaluated single-skill and multi-skill attack baselines. Meanwhile, ChainGuard reduces the attack success rate to 22.5% while allowing 99.5% of benign workflows to pass, highlighting the importance of chain-level security analysis for agent skill ecosystems.

</details>

TL;DR: 论文提出利用生成式人工智能构造高质量、类别均衡的合成加密网络流量数据，以缓解加密通信内容不可见及异常样本稀缺带来的分析困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密流量降低了传统基于载荷的分析可见性，而真实数据集中异常流量通常严重不足，导致分类模型训练偏置、泛化能力受限。因此，需要能够保留真实统计特征和变量关联关系的合成数据补充方案。

Method: 方法包括：对加密流量特征进行分析；通过基于聚类的数据生成策略构建合成样本；评估合成数据对原始数据统计性质与特征相关性的保持程度；并在分类任务中系统比较使用真实数据、合成数据及其组合训练的模型表现。论文同时提供了实现该流程的完整代码。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，结合特征分析与聚类生成的生成式人工智能方法可用于产生具有较高真实性和分类价值的加密流量数据。该方案尤其适合缓解异常样本代表性不足问题，但摘要未说明具体生成模型、数据集规模、评价指标及跨数据集泛化结果，仍需通过完整实验进一步验证其稳健性。

Abstract: Network traffic analysis faces significant challenges with encrypted communications, primarily due to limited visibility into packet contents and the inherent imbalance in available datasets, particularly for anomalous traffic patterns. This paper addresses these challenges by exploring Generative AI (GAI) techniques to create realistic and balanced synthetic encrypted traffic datasets. Our approach incorporates feature analysis, clustering-based data generation, and comprehensive classifier evaluation to ensure synthetic data quality. We demonstrate that properly generated synthetic data can effectively supplement real- world datasets, achieving up to 93% performance when training classifiers compared to those trained on real data. The proposed methodology preserves critical statistical properties and feature correlations while enabling the creation of balanced datasets, ad- dressing the persistent challenge of anomaly underrepresentation in cybersecurity data. Along with the results we provide complete programming code designed and implemented in this work.

</details>

*Jukka Ruohonen,Qusai Ramadan*

TL;DR: 本文对IETF RFC中的“安全性考虑”章节进行实证分析，发现安全讨论覆盖率较高，但强制性安全要求较少；引用网络整体稀疏且集中于少数RFC，讨论主题主要由具体协议的安全问题决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有标准化研究较少系统考察RFC安全性考虑章节的内容、引用关系及其长期演化。本文旨在填补这一研究空白，并评估互联网标准文档中安全议题的呈现方式与发展规律。

Method: 研究者对样本RFC的安全性考虑章节进行内容分析、纵向主题分析和RFC间引用网络分析，考察安全讨论的出现频率、强制性要求、章节引用结构、引用数量随时间的变化，以及不同安全主题与协议之间的关联。

Result: 超过90%的样本RFC在相关章节中明确讨论了安全问题，但强制性安全要求极少出现。安全性考虑章节构成的RFC引用网络较为稀疏，仅少数RFC及其安全章节被大量引用。引用数量大致在20世纪90年代中期至21世纪10年代中期达到高峰。讨论主题并非主要围绕一般性安全问题，而是更多反映特定协议的具体安全风险；除一般网络安全、安全规范和路由外，主题的长期演化也主要呈现协议特异性。

Conclusion: RFC安全性考虑章节已广泛纳入安全讨论，但其规范约束力有限，知识传播主要依赖少数高影响力文档。安全议题整体表现出显著的协议导向和路径依赖特征，研究结果为理解互联网标准化中的安全实践提供了新的实证证据。

Abstract: Request for comments (RFCs) are Internet standards, memorandums, and related technical documents about core Internet protocols made via and released by the Internet Engineering Task Force (IETF). In the early 1990s each RFC was required to have a section for security considerations. The present work examines these sections. According to the empirical results, (1) over 90% of the RFCs sampled have discussed security explicitly in these sections, (2) although mandatory security requirements have only seldom-if ever-been imposed. Furthermore, (3) the RFC-to-RFC reference network specific to the security consideration sections is sparse, although a few RFCs and their security consideration sections are heavily referenced. In addition, (4) the volume of references peaked during a period from circa mid-1990s to mid-2010s. Regarding the topics discussed in the sections, (5) these do not represent general security issues, such as spoofing or eavesdropping; rather, the topics mostly reflect distinct security issues specific to distinct protocols. With the exceptions of network security in general, security specifications, and routing, (6) also the longitudinal evolution of the topics is protocol-specific. As the subject matter has not been previously examined, these empirical results fill a gap in the standardization literature.

</details>

*Alexander Panfilov,David Schmotz,Ilia Shumailov,Luca Beurer-Kellner,Joachim Schaeffer,Ameya Prabhu,Jonas Geiping,Maksym Andriushchenko*

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种客户端推理链加密架构漏洞：同一服务商不同会话、用户和模型之间的加密推理块可互换，使攻击者能够借助较弱模型将高能力模型的加密推理解密并以明文输出。该漏洞可导致模型推理泄露、私人数据暴露、隐藏风险信息泄漏以及隐蔽提示注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型提供商为保护知识产权并减少信息泄漏而隐藏逐步推理过程，但将加密推理块返回客户端并要求后续请求携带，可能扩大攻击面。研究旨在评估这种客户端状态管理方式的隔离性与安全性。

Method: 研究者分析不同会话、用户及模型间加密推理块的兼容性，并构造跨模型解密攻击：将目标模型的加密推理块注入同一服务商的较弱模型，诱导其直接解码并输出明文。研究进一步在多个主流模型生态中验证推理提取、公开日志数据恢复、隐藏信息泄漏和加密块内隐蔽提示注入等攻击路径，并提出密码学与系统层面的防护方案。

Result: 实验表明，加密推理块在不同上下文之间具有可互换性，攻击者无需直接突破高能力模型即可提取其推理内容。研究覆盖Anthropic、OpenAI和Google等平台；从公开代码仓库中获取并解码315,320个推理块，恢复367项个人身份信息和182项凭据。该漏洞还可能暴露模型推理中的隐藏风险信息，并允许在加密块中嵌入不可见的提示注入载荷。

Conclusion: 客户端传输加密推理块若缺乏强会话绑定、模型绑定和完整性保护，可能形成严重的跨模型解密与数据泄漏风险。应采用不可跨会话或跨模型复用的密钥与认证机制、严格的上下文绑定、服务端最小化暴露策略、日志脱敏及加密状态完整性校验，以降低推理窃取和隐蔽注入风险。

Abstract: Leading large language model providers now conceal their models' step-by-step reasoning, or chain-of-thought, to protect intellectual property and limit information leakage. Rather than storing these traces server-side, providers return them to the client as blocks of encrypted text, which the client passes back with each subsequent request. Building on prior research, we identify an architectural vulnerability: these encrypted blocks are fully compatible and interchangeable across different sessions, users, and models within a provider's ecosystem. We exploit this compatibility to develop a scalable decryption jailbreak. By injecting an encrypted reasoning trace from a given model into a weaker, and less safeguarded model from the same provider, we force it to decode and output the trace verbatim in plaintext, without ever jailbreaking the more capable model directly. This vulnerability enables four distinct attack vectors. First, it circumvents anti-distillation mechanisms, allowing adversaries to extract a proprietary model's reasoning, as we demonstrate across Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google. Second, it allows for large-scale private data extraction. Developers frequently share session logs publicly, unaware of contents of the encrypted blocks. By decoding 315,320 reasoning blocks scraped from public repositories, we recovered 367 Personally Identifiable Information (PII) artifacts and 182 credentials. Third, it inadvertently reveals hazardous information hidden within the reasoning process, even in cases where the model's final, visible output safely rejects a malicious request. Fourth, attackers can leverage this flaw to execute invisible prompt injections, embedding malicious payloads entirely within encrypted blocks to poison public agentic rollouts. Following responsible disclosure, we propose concrete cryptographic and system-level mitigations to secure client-side reasoning.

</details>

*Gijung Lee,Ronald Wilson,Damon L. Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向硬件显微图像分析的隐私保护合成数据管线：先生成新的版图掩膜，再将其转换为逼真的SEM图像；仅使用合成数据训练的分割模型可迁移至真实图像，并优于使用少量真实数据训练的基线。


<details>

Motivation: SEM硬件验证需要大规模、高质量标注数据，但真实图像采集耗时，且其对应设计可能包含敏感知识产权。直接共享或训练于有限真实数据既难以扩展，也可能带来模型反演、成员推断等泄露风险。

Method: 方法采用两阶段生成框架：首先利用StyleGAN学习硬件布局掩膜的分布并生成宏观结构多样的新掩膜；随后使用条件生成对抗网络Pix2PixHD，将掩膜翻译为具有真实纹理和噪声特征的SEM图像。研究以这些合成图像训练分割模型，并在真实SEM图像上评估其跨域迁移性能；同时通过验证合成布局不复现原始专有布线来降低知识产权泄露风险。

Result: 实验的核心结果是，完全由合成数据训练的分割模型能够有效迁移到真实SEM图像，且性能超过仅基于有限真实数据训练的基线模型。合成版图在结构上具有新颖性，不包含原始设计中的特定专有路由。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，高保真且版图结构匿名化的合成SEM数据能够同时缓解数据稀缺与知识产权保护问题。两阶段生成策略为硬件显微图像分割提供了有效的仿真到真实训练路径，并有望降低部署模型时的数据泄露风险。

Abstract: Hardware assurance relies on scanning electron microscopy (SEM) to verify nanoscale structures, but assembling the large, high-quality datasets required for automated analysis is impeded by time-intensive acquisition and strict intellectual property (IP) constraints on proprietary designs. We propose a privacy-preserving pipeline that secures IP by heavily distorting the functional design while generating a visually realistic synthetic dataset from a small set of initial examples. A StyleGAN first learns the distribution of hardware layout masks to generate novel, macroscopically varied structures. Subsequently, a conditional GAN (Pix2PixHD) translates these masks into realistic SEM images that preserve authentic textures and noise. The primary finding of this work is that a segmentation model trained exclusively on this synthetic data not only demonstrates a successful "sim-to-real" transfer to real images but also outperforms a baseline model trained on the limited real dataset. Because the underlying synthetic layouts are demonstrably novel and reproduce none of the specific proprietary routing of the original design, deploying the final segmentation model mitigates the risk of exposing sensitive IP to attacks like gradient inversion and membership inference, providing a highly secure, high-performance solution for hardware assurance.

</details>

*Shabnam Ghasemirad,Christoph Sprenger,Si Liu,David Basin*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种形式化、系统化的数据库隔离性保证验证方法，覆盖七种隔离级别，并通过抽象模型层次结构显著降低证明复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库隔离性验证通常需要针对不同协议和较弱隔离级别分别开展复杂的归约证明，导致证明工作量大、复用性不足。本文旨在统一验证流程并减少用户所需承担的证明负担。

Method: 作者构建了包含多个抽象层次的模型体系，并系统研究隔离级别之间的验证差异：较强隔离级别可仅通过精化验证，较弱隔离级别则需要结合归约。通过提出通用归约理论，在高层抽象模型上一次性完成归约，从而避免针对每个具体协议重复证明。全部结果均在Isabelle/HOL中形式化。

Result: 该方法支持七种隔离级别，揭示了隔离级别在验证方式上的基本二分结构，并将复杂证明中最耗时的部分封装到抽象模型层次和通用归约理论中。最终，用户只需对层次结构中最具体的模型完成一次较为简单的精化证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个具有较强复用性和形式严谨性的数据库隔离性验证框架。其核心贡献是将弱隔离级别所需的归约证明提升为一次性的高层证明，从而大幅降低不同协议的验证成本，并为数据库并发控制性质的机械化验证提供了通用基础。

Abstract: We present a mathematically rigorous, systematic approach for the verification of database isolation guarantees, which (i) supports a spectrum of seven isolation levels, (ii) uncovers a fundamental dichotomy among isolation levels: stronger levels can be verified via refinement alone, whereas weaker levels additionally require reduction, and (iii) provides a hierarchy of abstract models that substantially simplifies proofs by factoring out their most labor-intensive parts. In particular, we eliminate the need for per-protocol reduction proofs for the weaker class of isolation levels by performing a once-and-for-all reduction at a high level of abstraction in our hierarchy. To achieve this, we develop and apply a generic theory of reduction, which is also of more general interest. Overall, our approach minimizes the user's proof effort to a single, simpler refinement of the most concrete model in our hierarchy. All our results are formalized in Isabelle/HOL.

</details>

Abstract: MetaML-style multi-stage programming (MSP) supports quasi-quotation-based code generation, runtime execution of generated code, and cross-stage persistence (CSP). However, its interaction with computational effects is subtle: mutable state can cause scope extrusion, where generated code escapes the scope of variables on which it depends.
  This paper presents a type system for MetaML-style MSP with mutable state that statically rules out harmful scope extrusion while supporting multi-level code generation, runtime execution, and a variant of CSP. Our system builds on refined environment classifiers (RECs), a discipline that annotates code types with the variable scopes on which generated code depends. To scale RECs to the MetaML-style setting, we refine classifiers so that they track not only variable scopes, but also the scopes of classifiers themselves. Further, we integrated polymorphism over classifiers, enabling more general and reusable code generation patterns in a multi-level setting.
  For the resulting system, we define an operational semantics via a definitional interpreter and prove type soundness and safety of offline code generation, showing that generated code can be extracted as standalone well-typed programs. We provide working implementations and mechanized proofs in Rocq.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum oracles are essential to many quantum algorithms, and their specifications may involve recursive control flow that depends on runtime quantum data. However, existing reversible compilation frameworks provide limited support for such quantum-controlled recursive structures.
  We present ReOC, a compilation framework that transforms high-level recursive oracle specifications with quantum control flow into reversible quantum programs. The framework comprises RQIMP, a high-level imperative source language for specifying recursive oracles, and a method of compiling programs in that language into RQC++, an existing high-level quantum recursive language with quantum control flow. In this way, we avoid the tedious and error-prone process of directly writing quantum oracles in RQC++.
  To manage static storage under dynamic quantum control, ReOC uses an indexed static-register discipline to isolate live variables across recursion layers, enabling safe register reuse while controlling quantum storage usage. Furthermore, to address the exponential time blow-up caused by naive uncomputation in recursive settings, ReOC employs a recursion-aware uncomputation strategy: temporary variables from recursive calls are cleaned using deferred strategies to control time overhead, while those from non-recursive statements are cleaned eagerly to reduce space usage. For linear recursion, this strategy yields overhead linear in recursion depth, parameterized by the per-layer register footprint and primitive-operation costs. Finally, we provide a mathematical proof of compilation correctness from RQIMP to RQC++, establishing semantic preservation and correct uncomputation of temporary quantum variables.

</details>

Abstract: Compiler bugs are pervasive in modern compiler systems, but the test programs that trigger them are often too large for practical debugging. Program reduction addresses this by minimizing test program size while preserving the original bug-triggering behavior. Existing approaches mainly rely on syntax-guided, rule-based deletion strategies that iteratively remove parts of the program in a trial-and-error manner. While effective in reduction quality, these approaches suffer from slow reduction speed.
  This paper presents SimP, a program reduction framework that combines traditional reduction with LLM-based syntax- and semantic-guided reduction. SimP leverages customized prompt design to guide the reduction process. SimP synergistically combines rule-based and LLM-based reduction stages to optimize the reduction performance. The results show that SimP improves reduction efficiency while achieving comparable reduction quality, with negligible LLM monetary cost.

</details>


### [53] [Bona: Automatic Management of Dirty Ancilla Borrowing in Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08765)
*Xiaoquan Xu,Chenke Liu,Boning Meng,Zihao Shen,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文研究量子线路中的脏辅助量子比特借用问题，证明该问题为NP-hard，并提出调度器\bona，通过深度感知启发式算法显著减少脏辅助比特需求，同时控制线路深度开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助量子比特管理直接影响量子线路宽度。脏辅助比特无需预先初始化、可借用暂时空闲且状态未知的量子比特，具有较高灵活性，但以往主要依赖人工处理，容易出错，且缺乏系统的优化方法。

Method: 首先形式化脏量子比特借用问题，并证明其NP-hard性；随后设计\bona调度器，采用深度感知的启发式算法，在量子线路执行过程中安排脏辅助比特的借用与归还。实验覆盖实际量子线路以及由真实线路模块随机组合生成的基准。

Result: 在多种基准上，\bona平均可减少约99%的脏辅助比特需求，同时保持可控的线路深度开销。在并行量子行走实验中，\bona达到与已有干净辅助比特方案相同的线路宽度，但线路深度显著更小。

Conclusion: 脏辅助比特借用具有NP-hard的优化复杂度，但通过深度感知调度仍可获得显著的实际收益。实验表明，脏辅助比特不仅能降低线路宽度，还能在具有特定并行结构的量子线路中实现优于干净辅助比特方案的深度效率。

Abstract: The management of ancilla qubits has become a critical technique for reducing quantum circuit width. Dirty ancillas, which may be borrowed from any temporarily idle qubit regardless of their initial states, offer substantial flexibility for width optimization, but their use has so far required manual and error-prone handling. We formalize the dirty-qubit borrowing problem and establish a fundamental computational limit by proving its NP-hardness. To support practical optimization, we present \bona, the first scheduler for dirty-qubit borrowing, built on a novel depth-aware heuristic algorithm. We evaluate \bona~ across a variety of benchmarks, including practical quantum circuits and randomly arranged compositions of real circuit modules, and find that it reduces nearly 99\% of dirty ancillas on average with controlled depth overhead. In particular, for parallel quantum walk---an essential component of parallel Hamiltonian simulation---\bona~ matches the circuit width achieved by the clean-qubit schemes of \citeauthor{jiang2024recycling}~(\citeyear{jiang2024recycling}) and \citeauthor{quantinuum}~(\citeyear{quantinuum}), but attains significantly smaller circuit depth, providing concrete evidence that dirty ancillas offer unique optimization advantages in circuits with certain parallelism.

</details>


### [54] [Sound Enforcement of Dynamic Release Information Flow Policy-Full Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09506)
*Jeffrey C. Ching,Danfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个能够静态检查并形式化保证动态发布策略的类型系统，解决信息流分析难以适应现实系统中动态安全策略的问题，并将其原型扩展到Rust语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息流分析通常假设安全策略静态不变，难以处理现实系统中随程序状态和业务流程变化而升级或降级的信息访问限制。尽管已有研究分别支持去分类、背书和调用策略，统一且强大的动态发布策略仍缺乏可靠的执行机制。

Method: 论文首先形式化支持动态发布策略的核心语言；随后设计用于检查该策略的类型系统；提出新的证明技术，并形式化证明类型系统的健全性，即类型检查通过的程序能够满足动态发布策略；最后将该类型系统实现为Rust语言扩展，并通过会议评审系统和Civitas开展案例研究。

Result: 研究得到首个可执行动态发布策略的类型系统，并证明其能够健全地约束信息流。原型实现表明，该方法可以集成到Rust中，并应用于具有动态信息释放需求的实际系统案例。摘要未报告具体的性能开销、实验规模或与其他方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了静态信息流安全理论与动态现实需求之间的部分鸿沟，为统一处理信息限制的升级与降级提供了形式化基础和工程实现。不过，其实际适用性仍需通过更大规模系统、更多策略类型及性能评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Information flow analysis is the de facto method of assessing confidentiality and integrity issues. However, the widespread adoption of information flow analysis in real-world systems is still lacking, partly due to a fundamental gap between theory and practice: the dynamic nature of security concerns in real-world systems goes beyond the scope of existing techniques that assume a static policy (i.e., data secrecy does not change).
  Recognizing the fundamental gap, a substantial amount of research has studied various aspects of it (e.g., enabling declassification, endorsement, and invocation policies). A recent work takes a step further by formalizing a promising end-to-end policy called dynamic release that unifies prior formalizations by allowing information flow restrictions to downgrade and upgrade in arbitrary ways. However, how to soundly enforce the powerful dynamic release policy is still an open question.
  In this paper, we present the first type system that enforces dynamic release policy and formally prove its soundness. More specifically, we (1) formalize a core language that enables dynamic release policy, (2) develop a type system that checks dynamic release policy, (3) develop new proof techniques and formally prove that the type system enforces dynamic release policy, and (4) implement a prototype of the type system as an extension to the Rust language, along with case studies on conference reviewing system and Civitas.

</details>


### [55] [Quantum Uncomputation of Clean and Dirty Ancilla Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09578)
*Chenke Liu,Li Zhou,Boning Meng*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算中的自动反计算，首次将干净辅助量子比特与脏辅助量子比特统一形式化，并提出两种面向综合的存在性检查方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动反计算研究主要针对干净辅助量子比特，未覆盖脏辅助量子比特，导致辅助量子比特的安全复用与正确释放缺乏统一理论和工具支持。

Method: 作者统一形式化干净与脏辅助量子比特的反计算问题，并证明判断反计算是否存在是coNP-hard问题。在此基础上提出基于重写的规范化算法RwUn，以及基于模板的推理系统TpUn，后者通过结构化的Store-Use模式保证反计算。两种方法均在Qiskit和Python中实现了原型。

Result: 与现有方法Reqomp相比，RwUn在实际复杂依赖基准上达到100%的覆盖率；在随机经典电路上的覆盖率约为其两倍；对于随机量子电路，RwUn在现有方法无法处理的范围内额外实现了约50%的覆盖，显示出更广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了自动反计算的理论边界，揭示了其计算复杂性，并通过RwUn和TpUn提供了统一且可实现的解决方案。实验结果表明，所提方法在多类电路上的覆盖能力明显优于现有技术。

Abstract: Automatic uncomputation aims to provide programming-language-level support to facilitate the correct and safe use of ancilla qubits in quantum computing, but efforts have only been made for clean ancillas, leaving dirty ancillas unexplored. We present a unified formalization of the uncomputation of both clean and dirty ancillas. For the first time, we prove that checking the existence of uncomputation is coNP-hard. We introduce two complementary synthesis-oriented existence-checking methods: a rewrite-based normalization algorithm (RwUn) and a template-based reasoning system (TpUn) that guarantees uncomputation through structured Store-Use patterns. We implement prototypes of both methods in Qiskit and Python. Compared to the state-of-the-art Reqomp~\cite{reqomp}, RwUn achieves 100% coverage on practical complex-dependency benchmarks, twice the coverage on random classical circuits, and about 50% coverage on random quantum circuits beyond the scope of existing methods, demonstrating broader applicability.

</details>


### [56] [Categorical Models of Amortized Cost: An Adjoint Relationship between Cost and Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09635)
*David Binder,David Corfield,Dominic Orchard,Vineet Rajani*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文为成本与势能类型系统建立统一的抽象指称语义框架，并给出三个具体模型实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本追踪系统通常只能描述计算的最坏情况成本；引入势能后可以表达摊销成本，但不同类型系统和语义模型之间缺乏统一的抽象刻画。本文旨在明确成本与势能指称模型必须满足的结构性条件。

Method: 以λ-amor为基础，将成本与势能建模为一对伴随的分次函子。其中，成本函子同时构成分次单子和相容的分次余单子。在此框架下，作者构造并分析三个实例：忽略类型系统所追踪成本的集合论模型；原λ-amor论文中的Kripke逻辑关系模型，并证明其可转化为该伴随模型的实例；以及基于成本幺半范畴上余预层的新模型，使用Day卷积及其右伴随解释积对和函数。

Result: 研究抽象出了成本与势能模型的统一代数结构，并证明λ-amor的既有Kripke逻辑关系模型符合该结构。同时，提出了一个基于余预层和Day卷积的新型指称模型，扩展了成本与势能类型系统的语义表达能力。

Conclusion: 成本和势能类型理论可以由伴随分次函子统一解释，而成本函子的分次单子与分次余单子结构分别支持计算成本和资源潜能的组合与管理。该框架为比较、验证和构造不同成本追踪语义模型提供了通用基础。

Abstract: Various type systems have been developed to track the cost $κ$ of a computation using a cost-tracking monad $M\ κτ$. On its own, this only tracks the worst-case cost of a computation. If we also want to track amortized cost, then we can add a type $[κ]τ$ which stores potential $κ$ with a type $τ$, together with operations for storing and releasing potential. In this work, we build on one such system, $λ$-amor: $λ$-amor allows to track cost and potential in the type system and subsumes effect and coeffect-based systems, call-by-value and call-by-name based languages. In this paper, we identify the abstract properties that denotational models of type theories for cost and potential have to satisfy: Cost and potential must be modelled by an adjoint pair of graded functors, where the functor modelling cost forms both a graded monad and a compatible graded comonad. We present three concrete instances of this general abstract scheme: (1) A simple set-theoretic model that ignores the cost tracked by the type system, (2) the Kripke logical relations model in the original $λ$-amor paper (which we show can be turned into an instance of the adjoint model), and (3) a novel model based on copresheaves on a monoidal category of costs, where we model pairs and functions by Day convolution and its right-adjoint.

</details>


### [57] [Abstract Compilation as Abstraction of Operator Semantics, applied to Cost Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09769)
*Louis Rustenholz,Alessio Mansutti,Pedro López-García,Félix Ridoux,Niki Vazou,Manuel V. Hermenegildo*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出“算子语义”作为连接程序语法与经典指称语义的中间表示，并据此构建高阶抽象域和范畴化框架，以支持模块化、精确且健全的抽象编译；其主要实例是递归程序的静态代价分析与最优递推式提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小不动点语义虽适于描述递归程序的含义，却会隐藏产生该不动点的递归结构。这使传统抽象解释较难直接、系统地利用程序的递归生成过程来构造精确的分析器，尤其是在高阶函数、一般函数未知量及复杂数据规模度量下。

Method: 作者将程序解释为算子，而非仅解释为算子的不动点结果，提出算子语义。随后为函数、算子及程序自身设计高阶抽象域，以“组合”作为核心抽象原语；并采用范畴论框架刻画抽象编译器的构造条件，保证其健全性、精确性与模块性。最后将框架实例化到基于递推式的静态代价分析，面向代数数据类型上的递归程序提取递推关系。

Result: 该方法实现了与具体求解器无关的递推式提取，并宣称具有最优性。其分析能力覆盖一般形式的函数未知量，以及基于折叠的度量函数，从而支持比传统大小度量更广泛的数据规模刻画。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是以保留递归构造结构的算子语义重构抽象编译视角，并以高阶抽象与范畴化构造方法提升分析器的可组合性和精度。递推式静态代价分析实例表明，该框架可扩展到复杂递归程序及丰富的规模度量场景。

Abstract: Least fixpoints are fundamental to program semantics, but they abstract away the recursive structure that generated them. We introduce operator semantics: a semantic intermediate representation between syntax and classical denotational semantics, which treats programs as operators. Abstract compilation is then understood as the act of abstracting such operators. We develop higher-order abstract domains for functions, operators, and programs themselves, in which composition is the key novel primitive, together with a categorical framework for constructing sound, precise, and modular abstract compilers. We instantiate this framework in the context of recurrence-based static cost analysis, developing solver-independent, optimal recurrence extraction techniques for recursive programs over algebraic data types, that support general function unknowns and catamorphic metrics, a broad class of size metrics beyond traditional approaches.

</details>

### [58] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张以计算论证为基础构建评估型人工智能（EAI），通过同时呈现相互竞争的假设及其支持与反对证据，辅助人类进行可解释、可质疑的决策，而非直接给出单一建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工智能决策系统往往输出单一结论，难以充分表达不确定性、权衡关系和异议空间。EAI旨在支持以人为中心的决策，因此需要一种能够形式化表示多种假设、证据及其冲突关系的理论与计算基础。

Method: 本文属于立场论文，提出将计算论证作为EAI的核心范式。该范式通过形式化建模论点、证据、支持与反驳关系，为系统提供可计算、可解释且可质疑的决策支持机制，并据此提出面向分布式和以人为中心EAI系统的长期研究议程。

Result: 文章的主要成果是论证了计算论证与EAI在目标和功能上的高度契合，明确了其在解释竞争性假设、呈现正反证据以及支持用户质疑方面的潜力。本文未报告具体实验、数据集或定量评估结果。

Conclusion: 计算论证可为可解释、可质疑的EAI提供适合的形式化基础，并有望推动分布式、以人为中心的评估型智能系统发展。后续研究需要进一步解决系统架构、交互机制、论证计算及实际应用验证等问题。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [59] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，在高风险领域，AI输出速度超过人类认知容量时，传统人工监督会失效；真正的约束是输出速度V与单项认知负荷L的乘积。作者提出不判断内容正确性的“Flow-by-Flow”治理框架，通过形式化认知成本、非线性流量惩罚和机构处理容量上限控制监督负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有治理依赖人工或AI判断输出是否正确，但前者受限于人类处理能力，后者会继承模型幻觉与误判风险。论文进一步指出，模型能力提升并不会整体降低监督负荷：分流成本和响应成本基本不变，判断成本的下降还可能表现为遗漏而非真实减负。因此，需要一种绕开逐项内容判断、直接控制输出流量和处理负荷的治理机制。

Method: 作者将单项认知负荷L分解为分流、判断和响应三部分，并分析其对模型能力提升的非对称响应。在此基础上提出Flow-by-Flow框架：使用基于形式化、可计数特征的认知成本评分，对高流量生产施加非线性成本；同时设置机构容量上限，将处理量控制在C_max以内。框架还提出四项设计不变量：不进行内容判断、不允许规模化消耗审查者能力、按身份绑定每次申请的摩擦成本、禁止批量放行。作者通过一个参考实现讨论这些不变量的可共同实现性，并采用1000组参数抽样的蒙特卡洛分析进行比较。

Result: 理论分析表明，能力提升主要重构而非消除监督负荷，基于正确性评估的治理方案面临AI误判风险或V×L容量上限。蒙特卡洛结果显示，复合型流量控制在90.8%的参数试验中优于单纯强化监督。参考实现说明四项设计不变量在原则上可以同时满足，但实际部署仍存在明显困难。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将治理重点从“判断每条内容是否正确”转向“控制内容流量及其对监督能力的消耗”。Flow-by-Flow为高吞吐AI系统提供了减少人工监督瓶颈的替代路径，但其有效性依赖于认知成本指标的可测性、容量上限的合理设定、身份绑定机制的抗规避能力，以及对实际误伤和部署复杂性的进一步验证。当前证据主要来自理论推导和参数模拟，尚不足以证明真实环境中的长期效果。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [60] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从复数结构理论构造规范解释的形式化框架，区分闭包稳定化、全局完备化和确定化三个层次，并分析基于算子与基于选择器的规范化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构理论可能产生多个彼此不一致或不可比较的解释，缺乏统一的规范化标准。本文旨在刻画非确定性的来源、规范化所需的结构条件，以及这些机制在推理系统和大语言模型中的适用性。

Method: 将结构理论形式化为签名、公理和推理策略的三元组，并以全局一致的结构结论分配定义可接受解释族。非确定性被划分为认识性多元（Type E）和结构性多元（Type S），其中Type S-strong进一步要求不存在公共上界。研究基于算子的完备化与基于选择器的构造，分析正向、非撤回规则下的饱和闭包算子，并利用分阶段算子研究多层规范化的非交换性。

Result: 证明了若干规范化机制的充分结构条件：Type E理论可实现闭包稳定化，但完全确定化依赖尚未解决的全局合流性；Type S-strong理论可通过规范选择实现确定化。纯推理式完备化在满足额外可靠性条件时可归约为饱和闭包算子，并给出将理论内在机制归类为闭包或选择的条件性分类定理。框架还将大语言模型中的幻觉解释为缺乏支持的规范化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一分析结构理论规范解释的框架，明确区分收敛、全局一致性与唯一性。主要贡献是揭示不同非确定性类型对应不同规范化路径，同时指出Type E理论实现完全确定化仍需解决全局合流性问题。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [61] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将基于效价—唤醒度的情感建模扩展到连续状态的主动推断驾驶模型中，并结合当前状态与未来预测结果估计驾驶情绪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推断驾驶模型主要关注目标导向行为与不确定性降低，尚未充分考虑会显著影响交通决策的情感状态；已有情感主动推断研究又多局限于离散状态和简化环境。

Method: 构建连续状态下的主动推断驾驶模型，扩展效价与唤醒度的计算方式，并使情感估计同时依赖当前状态和预测的未来结果；在两个交互式驾驶场景中进行评估。

Result: 模型生成的情绪信号呈现出与相关驾驶情境研究中报告的情感模式相一致的变化特征。

Conclusion: 该方法为连续状态主动推断驾驶模型引入了可解释的情感表征，表明结合未来结果的效价—唤醒度估计能够有效刻画驾驶过程中的情绪动态。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [62] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据中心并行（DCP），根据每个批次的序列长度动态调整并行规模、梯度累积和重计算等运行时配置，在兼顾效率与易用性的同时，最高实现2.88倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变量长度的长序列训练会导致静态配置下的工作负载不均衡和资源利用率低；而现有动态或复杂方案通常需要较大代码改动，难以适配新模型。本文旨在打破训练效率与使用便捷性之间的权衡。

Method: DCP以数据特征为运行时决策依据，根据每个批次的序列长度动态调整直接运行时设置，包括并行规模、梯度累积和激活重计算等。该方法强调通用性，可通过约10行代码集成到任意模型中。

Result: 在32张H200 GPU上的实验中，DCP最高获得2.88倍加速。摘要表明，该方法能够改善变量长序列训练中的负载均衡和计算效率，同时保持较低的集成成本。

Conclusion: DCP提供了一种简单、通用且有效的数据驱动运行时调度方案，缓解了变量长序列训练中效率与易用性的矛盾，有望作为分布式训练研究与实践的稳健基线。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [63] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，线性探针几乎能完美检测语言模型上下文损坏，但无法可靠预测最终答案是否正确；探针监控的有效性高度依赖模型、错误类型与干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将内部状态检测能力等同于失败预测能力，并假设持续检测到的损坏信号能够更好地区分正确与错误结果。本文检验这种关联及其对部署监控的实际意义。

Method: 在多跳算术推理链上，评估线性探针对上下文损坏的检测能力、最终答案正确性预测能力、结构化置信度输出及跨跳探针信号的持续性，并比较branch-and-pick、reprompt和replace-prior等基于探针的实时干预策略；实验覆盖多个模型家族及推理模型。

Result: 探针对上下文损坏的检测准确率接近完美，但对最终答案正确性缺乏信息量。强制模型输出结构化置信度后，置信度坍缩为两个数值，且两类结果的错误率无法区分；探针信号持续性也不能有效区分正确与错误结果，预注册的“持续性优于峰值”假设未获支持。干预方面，branch-and-pick在不同模型上总体有利，并在Llama-3.1-8B上实现4次纠正且未破坏任何正确轨迹；reprompt和replace-prior破坏正确轨迹的比例大致与纠正错误的比例相当。

Conclusion: 内部探针能够识别模型处于受损上下文，却不等于能够预测最终失败或表达可靠置信度。探针监控应作为语言化置信度的必要补充，但不存在普适最优干预方案；实际部署应依据模型和错误类型进行路由，并谨慎评估干预对正确推理轨迹的破坏风险。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [65] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多智能体大语言模型的任务条件路由框架，将智能体选择与通信建模为带激活成本和通信成本的合作博弈，并结合边际价值、贪心路由和Shapley值估计，在合成实验中以较少激活智能体达到接近最优的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体架构通常预先固定通信，或允许所有智能体广播，导致令牌消耗、延迟、冗余和错误传播随参与智能体及通信链路增加而上升。论文旨在根据任务动态选择有价值的智能体和通信边，从而在性能与系统成本之间取得更优平衡。

Method: 定义任务条件下的净效用U(C|x)=V(C|x)-Σc_i，将联盟价值、智能体激活成本和通信边成本分离建模。提出基于边际价值的激活规则与贪心路由器，并使用估计Shapley值预测执行前及执行中的潜在有价值智能体。理论上将问题联系到子模最大化：对单调且具基数约束的特殊情形给出曲率修正保证，对无约束非单调情形通过双重贪心给出带符号目标修正的紧致1/2近似；此外建立Shapley-子模性夹逼界，刻画边际价值路由误差与逐智能体递减收益之间的关系。

Result: 合成实验中，贪心路由达到穷举最优效用的99.5%，平均仅激活8个智能体中的1.96个；全量广播仅达到38.8%。方法对激活成本和冗余权重较稳健，但在强子模性违背或价值估计噪声较大时，性能下降至66%。理论保证不直接适用于论文主路由器，因此其核心方法仍属于启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的成本敏感型智能体选择与通信优化框架，实验显示其能够显著减少参与规模和通信开销，同时保持较高任务效用。主要局限是主路由器缺乏直接理论保证，结果目前主要来自合成实验，且依赖子模性假设与价值估计质量；后续应在真实多智能体大语言模型基准上验证泛化性、鲁棒性和实际令牌及延迟收益。

Abstract: Modern agentic AI systems combine multiple large language model agents with heterogeneous skills, yet most architectures either fix communication in advance or allow full broadcast. Both can be inefficient because token cost, latency, redundancy, and error propagation increase with the number of active agents and communication links. We model agent selection and communication as a cooperative game with task-conditioned net utility $U(C\mid x)=V(C\mid x)-\sum_{i\in C}c_i$, separating coalition-level costs from agent activation costs. We propose a marginal-value activation rule and greedy router, extend the model to optimize communication edges with per-edge costs, and use estimated Shapley values to predict which agents are worth contacting before and during execution. We connect the problem to submodular maximization and prove two limited guarantees: a curvature-refined bound for a monotone, cardinality-constrained special case, and a tight $1/2$-approximation, with a correction for signed objectives, for an unconstrained non-monotone case via double greedy. Neither guarantee applies directly to the main router, which remains a heuristic. We also prove a Shapley-submodularity sandwich bound linking the error of marginal-value routing to a per-agent diminishing-returns quantity. In synthetic experiments, greedy routing achieves $99.5%$ of brute-force-optimal utility while activating $1.96$ of $8$ agents on average, compared with $38.8%$ for full broadcast. Performance is robust to activation cost and redundancy weight but falls to $66%$ under strong violations of submodularity or noisy value estimates. We distinguish the framework from Shapley pricing, hedonic coalition formation, and communication-graph pruning, and propose evaluation on real multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [66] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSpace，一种基于变形测试的具身智能体空间认知评估框架，通过从真实执行轨迹中生成测试用例，自动检测空间认知错误。实验表明，当前主流多模态大语言模型驱动的具身智能体空间认知能力明显弱于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估主要依赖人工标注的视觉问答对或任务完成率。前者成本高且受标注质量影响，后者可能掩盖不安全行为、低效策略及任务执行过程中的空间认知缺陷。因此，需要一种能够直接评估智能体执行过程中空间认知鲁棒性的自动化方法。

Method: MetaSpace借鉴软件工程中的变形测试思想，从真实执行轨迹中提取时空多模态状态，并依据逻辑规则和物理规律预定义变形关系，自动生成测试用例。研究者使用Prolog将变形关系编码为可执行规则，通过检测关系违例识别空间认知错误，并提出Spatial Cognition（SC）评分量化智能体表现。

Result: 在三个具身场景中的实验发现，MetaSpace共检测出当前主流多模态大语言模型驱动智能体的90,422个空间认知错误。各类智能体的平均SC得分介于0.44至0.52之间，而人类基准为0.96，显示出显著差距。

Conclusion: MetaSpace能够以自动化、可解释的方式揭示传统任务成功率指标难以发现的空间认知缺陷。实验结果表明，现有具身智能体虽然可能完成部分任务，但其空间认知能力仍不稳健，未来评估应更加关注执行过程中的认知正确性与安全性。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution.
  Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>

Abstract: As LLM agents move from decision support to autonomous procurement, firms need to know whether delegated negotiators create value, divide it predictably, and avoid money-losing contracts. We study this in a canonical supply chain bargaining problem: a buyer with private demand information negotiates a quantity-payment contract with an uninformed seller. We benchmark nine LLMs from OpenAI, Google, and Alibaba against a validated Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium across 9,840 LLM-to-LLM negotiations. First, capability governs value creation. Agents agree in 98.9% of negotiations and capture 95.4% of first-best surplus undiscounted, but average 2.98 rounds against the benchmark's 1.25, and this delay erodes 21-34% of surplus. Capability also governs reliability: baseline models accept individually irrational contracts in 19.2% of cases, versus 0.0-0.6% at mid-tier and flagship, making automated profit verification the binding guardrail below that threshold. Second, surplus capture is relational. Provider identity predicts who captures surplus better than capability rank: self-play buyer shares average 40% for OpenAI, 50% for Google, and 70% for Alibaba's Qwen, an ordering that survives restricted communication and no discounting. Reversing which provider sells moves the division by 7-18 percentage points, and the capable Qwen flagship is the weakest cross-family seller: vendor choice is a first-order distributional decision. Third, the prompt is a strategic lever. Delegation separates the principal's economic patience from the agent's prompted strategic patience, a free deployment choice that is the single strongest driver of surplus division (90% of explained variance). Together these establish an equilibrium-referenced audit of AI agents along three dimensions: discounted efficiency, distributional profile, and operational reliability.

</details>


### [68] [TREAT: Evaluating Access to Formal Knowledge across Equivalent Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07540)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TREAT基准，用于评估大语言模型能否在定理条件经历等价但形式显著不同的变换后，仍正确识别其对应的标准定理身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的AI常需将灵活、多样的输入表述映射到下游工具所依赖的规范化形式对象。现有评测多关注文本改写下的理解，但较少检验模型面对公式层面等价变形时，是否能稳定恢复已知定理。该问题直接反映模型对形式知识的表征鲁棒性与可调用性。

Method: 作者构建TREAT数据集：从抓取的定理页面中筛选具有可用数学表达的条目，提取规范化的定理条件，并生成保持逻辑等价的变体。变换包括残差方程、见证变量陈述、优化恒等式、集合关系、算子形式及证明过程中的中间刻画。每个样本记录适用假设与逆映射，以支持可验证、可审计的评测。最终语料含737个定理身份与29,480条变换样本，任务目标是在变换后的条件下检索正确的定理身份。

Result: 测试结果显示，表现最好的模型仅在60.73%的样本上检索到正确定理。其他系统还呈现多种失败模式，包括拒绝作答、错误识别以及输出格式不合法。这表明，即使数学条件在逻辑上等价，模型对定理身份的访问仍可能因表示形式变化而显著退化。

Conclusion: TREAT揭示了当前模型的定理知识并非对等价表示天然稳健。该基准通过明确的目标对象、等价关系、验证流程和可审计评分，为研究形式知识的表示鲁棒性提供了受控测试平台；其设计思想也可迁移至其他要求稳定识别规范对象的领域。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly operate between flexible input representations and formal objects used by downstream tools. A key challenge is recognizing when an unfamiliar formulation denotes a known formal object. We study this challenge through theorem recognition: given an equivalence-preserving transformation of a theorem condition, a model must recover the theorem identity associated with the standard statement. We introduce TREAT, a benchmark for evaluating whether large language models can recover known theorem identities from equivalence-preserving formula-level transformations. Rather than paraphrasing theorem text, TREAT changes the mathematical form of theorem conditions themselves, expressing known results through residual equations, witness statements, optimization identities, set relations, operator forms, and proof-intermediate characterizations. Starting from scraped theorem pages, we filter for entries with usable mathematical expression forms, extract canonical theorem conditions, and generate transformed variants with recorded assumptions and inverse mappings. The final corpus contains 737 theorem identities and 29,480 transformed rows. On a test panel, the best model retrieves the correct theorem identity in only 60.73% of cases. Other systems reveal different failure modes, including abstention, wrong detection, and malformed outputs. These suggest that theorem knowledge can be fragile under equivalent changes in representation. TREAT therefore provides a controlled testbed for evaluating representation-robust access to formal knowledge, with broader relevance to domains that require stable target objects, explicit equivalence relations, validation procedures, and auditable scoring.

</details>


### [69] [An AI Scientist that Doesn't Drift: Taste, Structure, and Falsifiable Findings in a Quadruped Navigation Research Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07542)
*Yiwen Zhang,Eloise Zeng,Jaeha Lee,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四足机器人导航策略泛化研究的AI科学家系统，通过结构化实验流程与偏好知识图谱，减少大语言模型研究循环偏离原始假设的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大语言模型驱动的自主研究循环容易围绕局部优化指标反复改进，而不是检验最初提出的科学假设，因此需要机制来保持假设可追踪、实验判断客观，并引导研究方向。

Method: 系统基于autoresearch范式，引入三部分机制：不可变实验卡，以固定格式记录预测及结果，防止事后修改假设；职责受限的专业子代理，分别执行机械性研究任务；kkanbu偏好预言机，将用户研究偏好表示为类型化知识图谱，并成为唯一能够进行主观判断的组件。研究在11条研究流中运行完全相同的循环，分别设置启用和不启用kkanbu的两组实验。

Result: 两组系统都没有明显偏离假设检验目标，约四分之三的自身假设被证伪。无kkanbu组训练出了性能最优的策略。kkanbu主要改变研究方向而非最终得分：只有启用它的系统探索了测试时适应，并在其占优的研究流中提出了获胜设计，同时能够在不同研究流之间迁移经验，另一组则多次重复推导相似结论。

Conclusion: 结构化实验框架能够维持自主研究循环的诚实性和可追溯性，而偏好预言机主要负责决定探索方向、设计选择与跨任务知识迁移。该结果表明，研究流程控制与研究品味引导可以被明确分离。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops driven by large language models can run machine-learning experiments at scale but tend to drift toward local refinements of whichever metric they optimise rather than testing the hypotheses that motivate the experiments. We address this structurally and present an AI Scientist for studying generalisation in quadruped robot navigation policies in simulation. Building on the autoresearch paradigm of Karpathy, our loop adds three components: an immutable experiment card that pairs each iteration's prediction with its outcome under a fixed schema, so a falsified hypothesis cannot be retconned; specialised subagents restricted to mechanical roles; and kkanbu, a preference oracle that holds the user's research taste as a typed knowledge graph and is the only component permitted to make subjective judgements. To isolate the oracle we run the identical loop twice across eleven research streams, with and without kkanbu. Neither arm drifts: both falsify roughly three quarters of their own hypotheses, and the best trained policy comes from the oracle-less arm. What the oracle changes is direction, not score: it alone explores test-time adaptation, it authored the winning designs where its arm led, and it carried lessons across streams that the other arm repeatedly re-derived. The scaffold keeps the loop honest; kkanbu decides where it looks.

</details>


### [70] [The Field Knows: Cross-Dimensional Geometry from Navigation to Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07566)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由单一因果对比损失训练的连续度量场框架，可在不同维度中学习并生成从机器人导航几何到洛伦兹时空结构的多类几何现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建统一且可迁移的几何学习框架，使模型不依赖特定场景记忆，而是学习可泛化的度量结构与因果关系。

Method: 将场景编码为固定对称矩阵基的系数，组装为李代数元素，并通过指数映射生成黎曼或洛伦兹度量；训练过程中仅使用单一因果对比损失，并在不同维度和任务上采用相同架构与训练协议。

Result: 在平面导航、机械臂配置空间和洛伦兹时空等任务中，模型学习到避障测地线、事件视界及其他几何结构。零样本实验表明其具备跨配置的迁移能力；黑洞实验中还自发形成了具有正确洛伦兹符号的类黑洞结构。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种统一的连续度量场学习范式：单一因果目标即可驱动模型发现多种具有物理意义的几何现象。不过，摘要未提供定量指标、基线比较、实验规模或对稳定性与可复现性的详细说明。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous metric field framework trained by a single causal contrastive loss. The framework encodes a scene into coefficients of a fixed symmetric matrix basis, assembles them into a Lie algebra element, and exponentiates the result to a Riemannian or Lorentzian metric. Across dimensions, this field discovers the full spectrum of geometric structures: from obstacle-avoiding geodesics in robot navigation across planar and manipulator configuration spaces, to event horizons of black holes in Lorentzian spacetime. Extensive zero-shot generalization studies demonstrate that the field captures transferable geometric structure rather than memorizing specific configurations. In the black hole setting, the causal loss spontaneously evolves genuine black-hole-like structures with the correct Lorentzian signature. The same loss, the same architecture, and the same training protocol produce the full range of geometric phenomena across dimensions. The field knows geometry, and geometry knows physics.

</details>


### [71] [TeXFix-Bench: An Empirically Grounded Multi-Format Benchmark for LLM-Based Document Source Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07617)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*
